putrītriśūlabhṛtamīśamahaṃ praṇamya

ṛjusakṣepasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvasthāpanasaṅkulam [g: sadguṇamiti]

hitāya mandabuddhīnāṃ smaraṇāya manīṣiṇām

bahutantrāṇyato vīkṣya krameṇaikatra likhyate

tadā [mu. a, k: tantrā] gamānusāreṇa liṅgaṃ vakṣyāmi sāṃpratam

līyate pralaye yatra tadidaṃ liṅgamucyate

hīnādi [kh: hīmādi] siddhayastasmācchivapūjopacārataḥ

sarvajñaṃ sarvadaṃ śuddhaṃ śivatattvaṃ ca mokṣadam

ato narādibhistattu liṅgarūpamupāsyate

mahārājyapradā pūjā dhyānaṃ mokṣapradāyakam

tatphalaṃ dviguṇaṃ samyag liṅgaikasthāpanāpanāddhruvam

tadeva tṛṇakuḍyādyairdaridrāṇāmaśāṭhyataḥ

lakṣaṇaṃ liṅgadhāmādau narebhyo vakṣyate'dhunā [kh: sadā]

liṅgaṃ ca [gh, ṅ: liṅgārcā; kh: liṅgasya] mukhaliṅgaṃ ca puṃstriyau ca napuṃsakam

sūryāṣṭāṣṭāsrake [mū. a: puryaṣṭāṣṭā; g: sūryacchāyā] viṣṇurbrahmā vedāsrake vibhuḥ

(vyaktaṃ [kh: ayaṃ cāpacinhāntargataḥ pāṭhaḥ nāśti] mūrdhādisaṃyuktaṃ vyaktāvyaktaṃ dvirūpakam

bhuktimuktipradaṃ miśramityetadrūpato bhavet

śīghraṃ siddhikaraṃ yacca kāmyākāmyāya yadbhavet

kārukā mūrtipāścaiva vanayātrādikaṃ yathā

puṇyasthānavibhāgaśca vāstugrahaṇamarcanam

śivo liṅgavidhiryāgo dhvajaparyantakaṃ tathā

yatīnāmāśrayo yādṛk pratiṣṭhā pañcadhā ca yā

sthāpane yaśca saṃbhāra ityādyakhilamucyate

kṣudrakāmāya lohotthaṃ ratnajaṃ bahusiddhidam

khādhāreṇa vyavasthāpyaṃ naśvaraṃ dhātudārujam [mū. a, k, ṅ: naśvaratvāttu dārujam]

liṅgaṃ ca dvividhaṃ jñeyaṃ [mū. a, k: proktaṃ] calaṃ caivācalaṃ tathā

sthiralakṣmīpradaṃ haimaṃ rājataṃ caiva rājyadam

vidveṣakārakaṃ kāṃsyaṃ rītijaṃ śatruvālanam

aṣṭalohamayaṃ liṅgaṃ kuṣṭharogakṣayāvaham

sikthaṃ kṛtvā mṛdā liptvā taptvodvāsya ca kārukaḥ

hemāhibhyāṃ kramātāratāmravaṅgāraghoṣakaiḥ

kāṣṭhaṃ kṣiptvā dṛḍhaṃ tattu śilāghṛṣṭaṃ savālukam

tayā lavaṇapūrāmbu [mū. tayālabāla pūrāḍhyaṃ] yuktayā lepayedbudhaḥ

bālakuñcikayā [mū. -kāla] sāmyaṃ nayettatkramaśaḥ śanaiḥ

tato'numā [kh: tato'ntarmā] rjayedyatnānmaṇinā [gh: ye drūkṣā] madanāmbubhiḥ

rasaye [mū.: vāsayet] ttāmrajaṃ śilpī hāṭakena sucārūṇā

guru snigdhaṃ mṛdusparśe [ṅ: mṛduṃ pakve] ṣaḍvidhaṃ hemaśobhanam

dhātūdbhavaṃ samākhyātaṃ ratnajādi [mū. a, k: ratnajāpi] vadāmyataḥ

sukhakṛtpuṣparāgotthaṃ vaidūryaṃ śatrudarpahṛt

liṅgaṃ mārakataṃ puṣṭyai sphaṭikaṃ sarvakāmadam

vaidrumaṃ vaśyakamārthaṃ [g: vadhyakarmārthaṃ] kārketanamamitrahṛt

baladaṃ jīvajātījaṃ ṭaiṭṭibhaṃ śatrumardanam

iti prasthānaliṅgāni śuddhimānānyataḥ param

kārukai [kh: kārubhiḥ; mū. a, k: dārubhi] rghaṭitaṃ yacca talliṅgaṃ nirmitaṃ smṛtam

tatpūjyaṃ rājabhiryatnādbhuktimuktyarthibhiḥ sadā

ūrdhvādhovadanaṃ dīrghamadhaḥ sthūlaṃ ca niṣprabham

chidraṃ [g: sthiraṃ] ca sphuṭitaṃ trastaṃ [mū. a, k, kh: bhrastaṃ] śalyānvitaṃ [g, ṅ: śūlānvitaṃ] surojjhitam

rāṣṭragotrajanagrāma [ṅ: kulagrāma] dhanamitrasutānapi [mū, a, g: yutānapi]

rogaṃ parājayodvegaṃ [ṅ: parākramodvegaṃ] janadveṣaṃ kaliṃ tathā

tatsyāddoṣairvinirmuktaṃ dṛḍhaṃ snigdhaṃ prabhānvitam

mahāratnaṃ mahāmūlyaṃ guṇayuktaṃ yajetsadā

supratāpo [kh: saṃpratāpo] yaśaścāyurbhaveyurnṛpateściram

nīlaṃ pītaṃ sitaṃ cāpi śastaṃ ratnamanirmitam

śaṅkhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ javārambhādaladyuti

dhautāsyābho dvijātīnāṃ nṛpārhau [nṛpādau, li. pu. pā] pītaraktakau

talliṅgamāyuṣo vṛddhayai sarvarogavināśakam

śakrāgni(gnī) casamitroye [gh: śakrāgnicalamitrottha; ṅ: śakrāgnīndrasamitrāye] caturdhā tatkvacinmatam

śaradambunibhaṃ svacchaṃ snigdhaṃ ca sphāṭikaṃ hitam

sagarbhamūrmisaṃyuktaṃ jyotirmuktaghanaṃ [muktaṃ punaḥ, iti pāṭhāntara] tyajet

sthūlaṃ dīrgha kuśaṃ hrasvaṃ madhyādyanteṣu vā kṛśam [mū.: cākṛśam]

anyajātivilagnaṃ ca viddhaṃ bhītikaraṃ tyajet

sphāṭikaṃ liṅgamiṣṭārthaṃ mṛtyujitkāmadaṃ yajet

liṅgaṃ sadāgnihotrābhaṃ pūjya no doṣakṛnnṛṇām

sadā nimnena siddhiḥ syāducce tūccā same samā

ratnaliṅgaṃ dvidhākhyātaṃ svapīṭhaṃ dhātupīṭhajam [mū, a: sūpīṭhaṃ dhātupīṭhakam]

calaliṅgaṃ dvidhākāryaṃ dhāturatnamayaṃ śubham

kṣudraratnotthamapyevaṃ svamānā [g: pramāṇā] dvararatnajam

vaiśyānāṃ manmathāntaṃ syācchūdrāṇāṃ dvādaśāvadhiḥ [kh: khagagāvapi; gh: śāṅkagajāvadhi]

calaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ devānāṃ trayoviṃśatikāṅgulam

acalaṃ khadrūbhasthāyi karādanavasāmakam [mū, a, k, kh: sīsakam]

saḍḍhataṃ miśrakaṃ liṅgaṃ nordhvaṃ ca sthiramiṣyate

ṣaḍḍhastānnādhikaṃ vārkṣaṃ pārthivaṃ śailavatsadā

yakṣarakṣo'suravyāla gandharvāpsarasāṃ kvacit

pariṇāhocchrayābhyāṃ ca devānāmekaviṃśatiḥ

pañcāṅgulaṃtu paiṣṭyādyaṃ phalajaṃ svapramāṇataḥ

sthaṇḍile maṇḍale pūjya syāddīkṣāsthāpanādike [kh: -nānhike; gh: -nāntike]

prabhumānapradaṃ śāṅkhaṃ [kh: śārṅgaṃ] sainyacchinnāgadantajam

aṇimādinṛkulyotthamanyonyabdārdhataḥ [mū: nyaccharddhataḥ] sadā

śālipiṣṭodbhavaṃ puṣṭyai ṣāṣṭikaṃ puṣṭivardhanam [mū,: buddhivardhanam]

ripubhedakaraṃ maudgaṃ strīdaṃ śyāmākasambhavam

sukhakṛnnāvanītaṃ tu gomayaṃ roganāśanam

nānāphalodbhavaṃ [kh: lohabhavaṃ] liṅgaṃ nānākāmapradāyakam

uccāṭane tu pāṃśavyaṃ maulaṃ [ṅ: mārṣaṃ] śatrukṣayāvaham

purakṣobhakaraṃ pauraṃ [mū, a, k, kh, g: ghoraṃ] rāmaṭhaṃ caugryakārakam

mṛdbimbodbhavamiṣṭārthaṃ lepātsyuḥ sarvasaṃpadaḥ

tāvatkāla tu [g: tārako'nanta] saṃpūya visṛjya tāni [kh: visarjyaitāni] ke kṣipet

niṣkvāthya [mū: niṣkāṭhya; gh: niṣkāsya; ṅ: niṣkāma] paṭamālepya saṃśoṣya [g: saṃsodhya] pārimārjya ca

dhātubilvarasairlekhyaṃ tridhā liṅgaṃ paṭādike

balasaṃvartanīcchāyā dṛṣṭi [k, g, ṅ: sṛṣṭi] bhāvāśritā sadā

karuṇādbhutaśāntāśca navabhāvātmikāḥ kvacit

svecchayā pūjayedetanmṛnmayānyucyate [mū, a: dyucyate; kh: mayo hyucyate] 'dhunā

sadyomantreṇa cāpakvaṃ [ṅ: cāpaṅkaṃ] kramānnavakarādikam

dvijādijātibhirmṛdbhiravyaktādi ca kāmataḥ

vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu dīrghikāyāḥ samāharet

puṣṭyai trilohagarbhaṃ ca dhanakoṣavivṛddhaye

vāmākhyena citāṃ kṛtvā [ṅ: dhṛtvā] ghoreṇa paridāhya ca

īśānena gṛhītvā tu tataḥ saṃskṛtya pūjayet

sarjahiṅgupuraṃbola [puraḥ - gugguluḥ | puro guggulurākhyāto iti dharaṇi | bolaḥ - gandharasaḥ, bolagandharasaprāṇaḥ; ityamaraḥ] matasītailameva ca

mṛdi kṣiptvā vyavasthāpya māsaṃ tāṃ parimṛdya ca

śaṇatatvanvitaṃ [k: tatsūtritaṃ] katvā mṛnmayaṃ ghaṭayecchubham

ityeteṣāmabhāve tu rasendraṃ vajrasaṃyutam

gavyasarjayutaiścaiva lepyaṃ varṇakapuṣṭaye

sadvārarkṣartuyuktenhi cārulagnasamanvite

divyāntarikṣabhaumāni śubhāśubhaphalāptaye

ityādi śubhakṛddivyamato'śubhakaraṃ tvidam

digdāhastīvravātaśca candrasūryopamardanam [mū, a, kha: pasarjanam]

vāsavṛkṣāgrasaṃsthāyi [ṅ: śramasthāyi] balibhuṅmadhurārutiḥ

pāṃśvasṛgvarṣaṇaṃ ghoramāntarikṣamaśobhanam

dadhyakṣatayavā [mū, a: dadhyuddhṛtadharā] dūrvā dīpo gorocanā dhvajaḥ

govṛṣaśca kharo vājī bhasmākṣo bhūpatirdvipaḥ

śivaḥ śivodakuṃbhasthaḥ kauśiko vāmataḥ śubhaḥ

muktakeśaḥ parākṣepaḥ kṣāro'ṅgāraśca dāru ca

chinnāṅgo'locano rogī kapālī bhūprakampanaḥ

kṣutkṣāmonmattakau tantuścauṣadhīṣaṇasañcayaḥ [g: pañcamaḥ]

pakṣārdhārdhaṃ tu saṃtyajya prasthānaṃ kriyate śubhe

vedase syādvite [gh: yugāsrau syāddhitaiḥ] yātā tvagnidaṇḍānatikramāt

iṣṭādau [kh: indrādau] śaṅkaraṃ śastaṃ prāgīśenduvanaṃ [mū, a, k,: śendravanaṃ] vrajet

budhārkendusite jīve vārkṣe prāguttarasthite [gh: sthire]

ekāntarāsu [k: ekākarāsu] tārāsu śeṣe cāniṣṭakarmaṇi

niṣadho nīlagandhākhyaḥ śaṅgīketurnagādhipāḥ

nandākhyo malayo vindhya ujjayanto'tha [k: urjayanta] ṛkṣakaḥ [g: ṛṣyakaḥ]

yojanāni daśaiteṣāṃ girīṇāṃ nikaṭe vanam

etatkṣetrotthitā ye tu pāṣāṇāstaravaḥ śubhāḥ

sācāryaḥ sādhakaḥ śilpijanaiḥ parivataḥ śubhaiḥ

dhyātvādhaḥ prākṛtaṃ tattvaṃ śivatattvamayīṃ mahīm

diśo virecya cāstreṇa vighnanāśāya daiśikaḥ

mantrarakṣāvidhi kṛtvā saṃjapa [kh: saṃyajan] nniṣṭadevatām

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye liṅgadravyanirṇayavanapraveśo nāma prathamovidhiḥ

svarūpaṃ kathyate samyagiṣṭāniṣṭa ca karmaṇi

dhīraḥ śāstrārthakārī ca kartā yaḥ sātviko varaḥ

nāstikastārkikaḥ krūraḥ kartā yastāmaso'dhamaḥ

tasmātte guravo'nveṣyā ye tacchāstreṣu bhāṣitāḥ [g: preṣitāḥ]

antarvedīpratiṣṭhānaśrāvastyā [mū, a: cāraptyā; g: cārakṣyā; k: cāvantyā] guravo varāḥ

āṅgā jāṭodbhavāśceti [kh: āryajāto] saptaite madhyamāḥ smṛtāḥ

vīrajā drāviḍāpauṇḍrāstvaṣṭaiteguravo'dhamāḥ [gh: -tvaṣṭau te-]

vāṅgagāndhārapauliṅgā ete saptāntyajā matāḥ

saurāṣṭrā devarāṣṭrāśca aṣṭaite [k, kh, g, gh: aṣṭau] ninditā bhṛśam

adhamādyāḥ śubhe neṣṭā āryāvarte hi [kh: na] doṣadāḥ

yo deśo madhyabhāgaḥ syādāryāvartaḥ sa kīrtitaḥ

gotragocarasaṃyuktāḥ samayādyuktalakṣaṇāḥ

dīkṣāguṇenasaṃpūrṇāṃ [mū, a, k, ṅ: dīkṣāsthāpanasaṃyuktāṃ] ye jānantyatha saṃhitām

bhāryāvartodbhavāḥ śaivāḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ

pīṭhadhāmādikuṇḍajñāḥ pratiṣṭhāsthānavedakāḥ

lobhamohaparityaktāḥ śāntā dāntā vimatsarāḥ

sātvikā nipuṇā dakṣāḥ pañcaśrautārthavedakāḥ [k: -tattvārtha-]

dṛḍhavratājanadrohisatyavāgbhūtavatsalāḥ [g: vāgghatavāsanāḥ]

dīkṣitāḥ śivaśāstre smiṃścatvāro brāhmaṇādayaḥ

yojitaḥ śivatattve yaḥ śivarūpamavāptavān

śūdrādīnāṃ tathā mantrairyuktānāṃ śreṣṭhatā [mū, a, g, ṅ: hīnānāṃ śreṣṭhato] bhavet

tathaiva mantrasaṃyogāccāturvarṇaḥ śivī bhavet

dīkṣāyogāttatheśasya śūdrādiḥ śivatāṃ vrajet

yathā kulālacakrastho'vicchinnaṃ bhramate ghaṭaḥ

malaṃ viṣamadaṃ yadvaddhanti vai mantratejasā

syātkatakaphalairyogāttadvanmantraistu mānavaḥ

tathaiva mantrasāmarthyānmalaṃ hatvā śivī bhavet

tatheśamantrasaṃparkā [mū, a, k, g: saṃyogā] cchivatvaṃ jāyate paśoḥ

śaivācāryāḥ prayacchanti śivatvaṃ sa carācare

ācāryo'pi [kh: mantra tantra] tathaivātra śivatvaṃ yāti mantravit

saṃskriyante hi mantraiśca devācāryatapasvinaḥ

yathāpūjyaveda [gh: -pūjama-] jñastathā śudro'pi daiśikaḥ

sa śivaḥ svayameva syānnānyarūpaḥ kadācana

mantrayogādvibudhyante naṣṭāniṣṭaṃ tathā śive

śaivācāryaḥ [mū, a, k: śaivācāryāḥ] paraṃ sthānaṃ tadvattā [kh: tattvato] nmantranaukayā

śeṣe tvekaikahāniḥ syātsahasre ca śatakramāt [mū, a, k: dveśate kramāt]

pitāmahaśataṃ yāvadviṣṇormānaṃ vidhīyate

vinaśyanti tathopendrā asaṃkhyātāḥ pitāmahāḥ

sādākhyanimiṣeṇeśo layaṃ yāti na saṃśayḥ

śivātparataraṃ nāsti iti śāstreṣu niścayaḥ

tasmācchivadito mantraḥ siddhimuktiphalapradaḥ

mantraiḥ śivādhvare [k: śivapure; kh, g: śivāpure] jātaḥ sa śūdraḥ syātkathaṃ punaḥ

tathaiva pāpahānaṃ ca prākṛtāstu prajāyate [k: prākṛtaṃ tu na jāyate; kh: na prakṛtyāstu jātaḥ]

lakṣyā [mū, a, k, kh: loke] viprādikācārānnārakīṇo na tārakāḥ [kh: kārakāḥ]

na bhaveyustadā nūnaṃ dvijā devādidaiśikāḥ

viṣabhūtagrahādyāśca nigṛhyante hi mantrataḥ

carācaraṃ prapadyeta śivatvaṃ tatkṣaṇāddhruvam

etatsiddhaṃ sadā śaivaṃ gururūpamajanmakam [kh: gururūpamayātmakam]

śivāntanāmakāḥ sarve bhasmāṅgā [mū, a, kh, gh, ṅ: bhasmākṣā] brahmacāriṇaḥ

viśeṣayātu sāmānyā gururapyevameva tu

ambhojaṃ paṅkajaṃ yadvattadvacchaivā na cetare

tathaiva śivaliṅgānāṃ śaivāḥ pāśupatādayaḥ

liṅgabhedaphalairyukto yātyasau rauravaṃ dhruvam

anyathā pāpakārī syādityājñā pārameśvarī

brahmacārīgṛhasthatvabhedabhinnaṃ dvidhā sthitam

kāmyakāmikriyāṃ vacmi sāmprataṃ saṃprabhedataḥ

bhuktimuktīcchukaḥ kāmī kuryāddīkṣādimājñayā

pratiṣṭhādikriyāśeṣā tatkṛtā saṃpraśasyate

śivādhikāravṛttiryaḥ sa sāntānika ucyate

paścātkuryādvivāhaṃ ca gṛhasthaḥ sa vatārpakaḥ

bhaikṣyāśī saṃyatātmā ca vratī sa parikīrtitaḥ

puṣpapātābhidhānaṃ tu maṭhī [mū, g: mathī; k: sacī] mudrābhiriṣyate

sadvastramañjanaṃ tailaṃ nayate ramate hi tam [mū, a, k: ramaterhitaḥ; kh: ramatordhvataḥ; gh: rayaterhitam]

mākṣāya vratinaṃ caiva varayenmatimānnaraḥ

laukiko dravyabhāgī syādvidyābhāk pāralaukikaḥ

bhrātṛṇaṃ gurvabhāve syādgṛhasthānāṃ svaputrakam [mū, a, k: svaputragam; kh: suputrakaḥ]

ayogyāḥ śivakāryeṣu ghaṇṭāsambandhino haret [g: ghaṇṭāmayuktilohavat]

ṛṇe dravye ca piṇḍe ca sa evaikaḥ praśasyate

caturgocaramadhye ca śaivo jyeṣṭhāyate [mū, a, k, g: jyeṣṭhoyateḥ] param

śaivācāryastataḥ śreṣṭhaḥ sthāpayetsarvadevatāḥ [saha devatā, iti pāṭhāntaram]

bhuktimuktipradātā ca nānyo vai viśvakṛdguruḥ

tadabhāve [gh: vadadāve] tu kartavyāḥ svadevavratacāriṇaḥ [kh: vratadhāriṇaḥ]

śrutena śrotriyaścaiva brāhmaṇo brahmacaryataḥ

manuṣyajātisāmānye ko vidveṣaḥ śivadvije

na tathā brāhmaṇādīnāṃ tasmājjātirnakāraṇam [g: prakāraṇam]

upavītaṃ sadā dhāryaṃ śaivācāryairna cetaraiḥ

sāvitra itthamī parṇā proktā nānā śivāgame

dvayoḥ syājjyātirityuktaḥ sāvitrastu svagocaraḥ

dhvajaiśchatrairyutāḥ sarve śaivācāryā nṛpā iva

syādvṛddhirvijayaḥ [gh: sthāpiterdevaistatkartā ca] śāntirārogyaṃ dharmavardhanam

āyuṣā yaśasā lakṣmyā [gh: lakṣmī] putrapautrairvivardhate [gh: pravardhate]

tāvadya gasahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate

nākāle mriyate kartā parataḥ śivatāṃ vrajet

padaṃ saṃprāpya rudrasya sukhamakṣayamaśnute

tāṃstu sarvān prayuñjīta krūrakarmasu sarvadā

vikṛtāḥ śavalā [kh: sacarā] raudrāḥ śaṭhāḥ krūrāḥ [mū: sevā] kuvṛttikāḥ

vākkaṇṭhā baghiraḥ sthūlāḥ kunakhāśca kumūrdhajāḥ

purodhāḥ paṇyajīvī [gh: ścānyajīvī] ca vaṇijo dhātuvādinaḥ

vakranāsāṅgalamboṣṭhā mūrkhāḥ spandanagātrakāḥ

granthaviplākā [kh: viprocakā] vṛddhā vyasanī samayacyutaḥ

etaistu sthāpitā devā dhanāyuḥ putranāśakāḥ

katthakāḥ [kh, g, gh: kaṇṭhakā] kauśikā [k, g: kaulikā] stajjāstrikarṇādyāśca varjitāḥ

sanyāsavratinaścānye sthāpane nādhikāriṇaḥ

nirmālyabhakṣyaṇārthaṃ tu śāmbenācyutasūnunā

śivādīn sthāpayennityaṃ svayambhūḥ sa narādhamaḥ

sa punarbhūḥ parijñeyo varjanīyaḥ śivāgame

kuṇḍaḥ krūrasvabhāvaḥsyānmṛte patyau tu golakaḥ

prāvrājyānnaśyate [mū, a, kh: duṣyate] pūrvaṃ strīyogādaparaṃ tathā

trikarṇotthāstrikarṇāśca trikapānātma [mū, a: trikarṇastu tri] karṇakā

bhasmāṅkurastu [g: gurustu] katthosthastatsutāḥ kauśikādayaḥ

rāṣṭrakṣayakarā hyete ye ca paṣāṇḍino narāḥ

śāstrāvalokanādeva caṇḍeśastasya śāsakaḥ

sa yāti narakaṃ sadyo yājñikaiḥ saha daiśikaḥ

teṣāmācaraṇānnityamācārya iti kīrtyate

tasmānna mānavīṃ buddhiṃ kārayeddaiśikaṃ prati

pañcavaktrāṇi devasya pañcāgamavivṛddhaye

dīptaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ca sāhasramaṃśuma [kh: asumat] tsuprabhaṃ daśa

vijayaṃ pāramaiśvaryaṃ niśvāsodgītasaṃjñakam

narasiṃhaṃ candrahāsaṃ vīraṃ svāyambhurauravam [kh: rocanam]

([cāpacinhāntargato'yaṃ pāṭhaḥ ka pustake nāsti] rudrabhedāḥ samākhyātā daśāṣṭādaśa cordhvagāḥ

pañcabhūta [mū, a: pañcāmṛta] vibhāgaṃ ca śūnyabheda [kh: -mada] vinirṇayam

kambojaṃ kambalaṃ kaṅkaṃ kālakuṇḍaṃ kaṭāhakam

gāruḍasya tvime bhedā aṣṭāviṃśatirīritāḥ

asitāṅgaṃ ruruṃ caiva kapālīśaṃ samuccayam

bhīmāṅgaṃ ḍāmarārāvaṃ [kh: -rāgaṃ] bhīmaṃ vetālamardakam

triśiraṃ caikapādaṃ tu siddhayogeśvarīmatam

viśvarikaṇṭha saṅkocaṃ [mū: vikaṇṭha; kh: jhaṅkāra] tilakodyānabhairavam

catuḥ ṣaṣṭipramāṇāni dakṣiṇāsyādbhavāni ca

karapūjāvidhānaṃ tu vīṇātantraṃ taduttaram

siddhanityodayaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ cintāmaṇimahodayam

ānandarudrabhadrākhyaṃ [kh: bhadrākṣe] kiṅkaraṃ kiṅkarānugam

vāmadaṃ vāmadevoktaṃ [mū, a, k: devotthaṃ] caturviṃśatibhedagam [k: bhedajam]

karoṭaṃ [mū, a, g: kavāṭaṃ; k: kalphoṭaṃ] muṇḍamālaṃ ca kaṅkoṭaṃ khaḍgarāvaṇam [mū, a: vāraṇam]

ghorāṭṭahāsamucchiṣṭaṃ ghurghuraṃ duṣṭatrāsakam [mū, a: duṣkṛtāsanam]

yamaghaṇṭaṃ ca bhūtāhvaṃ [mū: bhūtāṅgaṃ; kh: bhūtājyaṃ] viṣabhedamajātajam

punarlakṣavibhedana grabhinnaḥ śāstrasaṅgrahaḥ

ṛṣibhyo mānuṣāḥ śiṣyāḥ sarvaśāstrāvatārakāḥ

iti sāmayikaṃ proktaṃ śambhunāgamapañcakam

ekatrabhojanaṃ ghore vāme vāmāmṛtaṃ tathā

ācaret samayān pañca yaḥ sa ācārya ucyate

tadavaśyaṃ hi kartavyamanyathā patanaṃ bhavet

ṣaṭkarmāṇi tu kāryāṇi śivācāryeṇa no paraiḥ

dīkṣāṃ ca sthāpanaṃ ceti [mū: caiva] ṣaṭkarmāṇi sadā guroḥ

krośān pañca parityajya gurvādeśāttadācaret

sādhakaḥ sādhayetsiddhirācāryo dīkṣayetpaśūn

pratiṣṭhādikriyā tasya na pāṭhādagra [mū, a: pāṭhānnaivāgra; kh: nayāvadagra; g: yāvanta vipra] janmanaḥ

yuktiḥ saṃsūcyate kācidyā coktā pārameśvare

nāśmādau śivatā tatra hyāṃśave dūrataḥ śivaḥ

cāṇḍālo'sāvabhojyānnaḥ pātakī nātra saṃśayaḥ [kh, gh: nārakī bhavet]

anyagotre [mū, a: śrotre; k: tyāśre] kuśā [kh: tre'ṅkuśā] pātānnārakī syād dvijādhamaḥ

p. 34) liṅgādisthāpakā ye ca te cāṇḍālasamā dvijāḥ

mantavyo vipravacchūdraḥ śūdrātpāpatarastu saḥ

vṛttātsiddhā vasiṣṭhādyā vyāsenoktaṃ ca bhārate

narake patati kṣipraṃ kartā svapitṛbhiḥ saha

svabrāhmaṇyaṃ gataṃ teṣāṃ brahmahatyāṃ [brahmahatvaṃ, li, pu] ca saṃsthitam

te krūrā dhvaṃsakā jñeyā yajamānakṣayāvahāḥ

dānārcanādyanarhaḥ syādvinaṣṭā brāhmaṇī [mū, a, k, kh, g, ṅ: naṣṭo brāhmaṇo] yathā

tadvadvedāḥ kuviprasthāḥ kartuḥ syuraśubhapradāḥ

ete śaivāḥ samākhyātā na tvete brāhmaṇāḥ kvacit

saṃskṛtānāṃ yatasteṣāṃ naśyante pūrvajātayaḥ

p. 35) pṛthaṅmantrāgamāveṣāṃ [mū, a, k, kh: -ścaiṣāṃ] vratāni kratavaḥ pṛthak

mugdhāḥ kurvanti ye viprāḥ śivādisthāpanaṃ kalau

tathaiva śivayāgeṣu na mantrā vaidikādayaḥ

na dvāraṃ maṇḍapādiśca sthāpanaṃ na śivādike

tasmācchaivena mantreṇa pratiṣṭheta [mū, a: pratiṣṭheyaṃ] śivādikam

śivamantraistayoḥ samyak śivatvaṃ nānyamantrataḥ

nānyadarśanamantrairvā pratiṣṭhā syācchivasya tu

na ca śaivīkriyā vede na ca khaḍgadisiddhayaḥ

agniṣṭomādayo yajñā vedoktā īśvareṇa tu

siddhāntasthāpanaṃ dīkṣāṃ noktaṃ vedeṣu śaṃbhunā

svamantrabījajā devā na bavhakṣaraḍambarāt [mū, a, k, g: na ca hṛtkaṇṭhaḍambaraiḥ]

naite śivādhvatattvajñāḥ stāvakā janarañjakāḥ

stutyaiva sthāpanaṃ na syācchivaliṅge calācale [carācare, li. pu. pā.]

śaivācāryaistu sā kāryā rudrabrahmārkacakriṇām

yakṣarākṣasayoginyo gaṇā gauryastathāparāḥ

svasaṃvedye [kh: sukhaṃ vede] 'pi rāgāndhā dvijā īśvaranindakāḥ

te'pi tyājyāḥ pratiṣṭhādau dūrādbauddhadvijādayaḥ

śivādisthāpanaṃ kāryaṃ nānyaistu brāhmaṇādibhiḥ

p. 37) svakarmābhiratā viprāḥ śaivādyāḥ kāpilāntakāḥ

svaṣaṭkarmānvito nityaṃ nānyakarmā dvijo bhavet

adhyāpanamiti syuśca ṣaṭkarmāṇi sadā dvije

tṛṇādimūlyabhikṣānnavṛttiḥ syābrāhmaṇo varaḥ

ādeśī vedavikretā ṣaḍete śūdravaddvijāḥ

manurdakṣo vasiṣṭho'triḥ kātyāyano'tha gautamaḥ

śaṅkhalikhitahārītaśātātapaparāśarāḥ

na śaivāditipraṣṭhāyāmete niprā niyāmakāḥ

devyā mataṃ mayaṃ nandiṃ pratiṣṭhā pārameśvaram

śakākhyaṃ vāmadevaṃ ca dvidhā paitāmahaṃ tathā

pratiṣṭhātantrāṇyetāni śivoktānyekaviṃśatiḥ

padmotthamārkaṇḍeyābhyāṃ rambhā [kh: gaurī] siddhādi rambhataḥ [mū, a, k, g: sauryataḥ]

mārtaṇḍāhrayato [kh, g: mārkaṇḍā-] lakṣyaṃ sarvametatsamuccayam [g: sarvatatva-]

śivāśramasthitaiḥ śaivaiḥ pratiṣṭhā nānyadarśanaiḥ

śaivāstanna namasyanti sthāpitaṃ cānyadarśanaiḥ

śaivasaṃskārahīnastu umābhyāṃ patati dvijaḥ

kṛtaṃ yadvratabandhādi tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhaven

na bhavedīpsitaṃ tatra vṛthaivāsau phariśramaḥ

śivoktaiśca vinā mantraistadetatprāpyate katham

miśrairvāpyatha doṣādya [k, g: doṣāḍhyaṃ] tkumāradyuktaśāstrataḥ

p. 39) karmacaṇḍaṃ bhavettasya karma caṇḍālakaśca saḥ

muktisiddhaṃ [mū, a, kh: yuktisiddhaṃ] hitaṃ loke skandoktaṃ cātra likhyate

kriyā sā na bhavetsādhvī mantrasaṃkaradūṣitā

agniṣṭomādayo yadvatpratiṣṭhāpīṣyate tathā

svargalokaphalā sādhvī vijñeyā vaidikīkriyā

vānaspatyaṃ samāsādya saṃsarantīha yājñikāḥ

punaḥ puṇyakriyākṣīṇaḥ sthāvarādiṣu jāyate

tasmātte munayaḥ sarva śaivārthe na viśāradāḥ

trayāṇāṃ brahmaṇādīnāṃ vedavidyāpracoditā

yajñe carūpuroḍāśapañcagavyādibhiḥ saha

p. 40) śūdrādīnāṃ ca yo dadyādvedavidyāprasādhitam

tasmācchaivo vidhiḥ prājñaiḥ śaivairmantraiḥprasādhitaḥ

sarveṣāmapi varṇānāmanu grahakaraḥ paraḥ

koṭikharvāvja vṛndaṃ ca mantrāstvīśādvinirgatāḥ

ayutāni ca pañcāśanmantrā vaktrā [g: rudrā] dviniḥsṛtāḥ

niyutāni ca ṣaṭtriśanmantrā rudrādvinirgatāḥ

koṭiśatāni pañcāśanmantrā vāmādviniḥsṛtāḥ

śataṃ sahasalakṣaṃ ca sadyojātādvinirgatāḥ

taistu liṅgādi saṃsthāpyaṃ nānyairvedādicoditaiḥ

taiḥ kṛtaṃ pañcadhā śāstraṃ śaivākhyaṃ siddhimuktidam

nimatsarā vadantyāśu [kh: drava] siddhāḥ [kh: śivāḥ] śavoktamantrataḥ

samyagvidhānattvajñairdīkṣito mucyate paśuḥ

sarvānugrāhakā mantrāścaturvarga [k, g: caturdaśa] prasādhakāḥ

prāgyāmyendujalāśāsyairlakṣagranthaiḥ kṛtā śrutiḥ

siddhimuktī vinā sarvaṃ śabdamātraparā matāḥ

īśānoktā śrutiḥ paścāt svargākhyaphalasādhane

avadhūtoktito'jñatvaṃ brahmaviṣṇornamadvaraḥ [kh: rasaṅgatam]

viṣṇuḥ pitāmahayutaḥ kimatāparasya

p. 42) gonarāśvādikaṃ hatvā yajvanāṃ narako bhavet

prāhuḥṣaḍaṅgāni maheśvarasya

īśvaraṃ kāraṇa nyāyādvadanti tarkavādinaḥ

śatāniṃdaśa mantrāṇāṃ brāhmaṇo dvisahasrakaḥ

ekonedve sahasre ca mantrāṇāṃ yajuṣastathā

kāṇva [k, kh, gh: kārṇa] madhyandināsaṃjñā kaṭhī prācyakaṭhī tathā

vaijavāpa neketyādyāḥ śākhā yajuṣi saṃsthitāḥ

gānānyapi ca catvāri veda āraṇyakastathā

p. 43) santuḥ śatakāścai brahmaprabhavakāḥ [gh: haṭakāḥ] smṛtāḥ

śaunakaḥ pippalādaśca muñjakeśādayo'pare

mantrāṇā yutaṃ sāṣṭaśaṃtaṃ copari ṣaṭśatam [gh: copaniṣacchatama]

śivādīnāṃ prayatnena hyanyathā doṣadāyakāḥ

rāṣṭrarājñobhaveddoṣaḥ [gh: rāṣṭra rājño bhave-] kartu kārayitustathā

nārakāste[kh: no rakṣiṇaḥ; gh: nārakinaḥ] samākhyātāḥ kartuḥ kulānvakārakāḥ

saṃkīrṇavṛttirvijñeyastvabhojyaḥ sarvadā budhaiḥ

bahurūpasya mantrasya nirdoṣo vai bhavettataḥ

ācāryo gīyate loke na hyasau pāramārthikaḥ

p. 44) daiśikāstrividhā grāhyoḥprāguktā ye gurukramāt

śivatulyo'pi sa tyājyodagdhahasto [mū: grāhyo-] guruḥsa hi

pratiṣṭheyuḥ surānsarvānabhāve daiśikasya tu

ṛjurlajjānvitaḥ sādhurjitakrodho jitendriyaḥ

sudṛṣṭiḥ [k, g: atṛṣṇiḥ] kulajo dhīmānnodvegī sūkta [gh: sūtra] tatparaḥ

prāsādaṃ maṇḍapaṃ dvāraṃ vidyādyo [vindyādyo, liṃ pu. pā.] hi sa [g: hema] kārukaḥ

śivadharma [gh: karma] vidhānajñā mūrtipā daiśikādayaḥ

vinītā nipuṇā dakṣā gurorādeśavartinaḥ [k, kh: rājñānuvartinaḥ]

iti lakṣaṇasārasamuccaye karturācāryādiparīkṣā nāma dvitīyo vidhiḥ

śilā vā jāti [mū, a, k, g: rājābhi] bhedena adhunaiva nirūpyate [kh: nirūpyante'dhunocyate]

kāśmīrī prāci yāmye vā khadiro brahmavṛkṣakaḥ

antargata jalastokaṃ sāgneyī rūpato dharā [kh: parā]

saumye toyaṃ ca māhendrī pīlūddālasnuhīyutā [g: dvālastu saṃyutā]

atṛṇāmbuśagālāḍhyā vāyavī sā mahī smṛtā

māhendrīvāruṇījātāḥ śastrāścānye ca garhitāḥ

śamīdrākṣā surāhvaśca navaite vipraśāntidāḥ

p. 46) kadarā [kadaraḥ=śvetakhadiraḥ, iti vācaspatyam] śokaśākaṃ ca śṛṅgī [g: vṛkṣī] ca nṛpabhūtidā

saralaḥ panaso bīja ityete vaiśyavṛddhidāḥ

kāñcanāsana [kh: mara] lodhrāśca sāla śūdrasyapuṣṭidāḥ

subhūsthāḥ [g: asvasthāḥ] śobhanā hṛdyā, ārogyā yauvanodvahāḥ

sāramadhyā draḍhīyāṃso nānyakāryeṣu yojitāḥ

granthitulyaśirāyuktān bṛddhabālāturāṃstarūn

valmikotthāñśmaśānotthān phalāmbhaḥ sevitāṃstyajet

kharitsaṅgamadevotthāṃstāpasāśramajāṃstyajet [mū, a, k: śrayajāṃ-]

acireṇaiva kālena cālako na bhavedasau

p. 47) devakārye na doṣaḥ syādityuktaṃ pārameśvare

śodhayedbhūmibhāgaṃ tu caturdikṣu samantataḥ

tataḥ śilpipradhānāśca sahāyāḥkusumādibhiḥ

viparīta tadā mantrī dūratastaṃ vivarjayet

gomayena tato bhūmi lepayitvā gurustadā

dhyātvā devaṃ ca vijñāpya kṛtvācārghyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ [k: kṛtvācāyaṃ vicakṣaṇam]

astraṃ dhyātvā yajenmantrī [kh: japen-] varmaṇā [mū: carmaṇā] pariveṣṭayet

śiva saṃpūjya vahnistha navadyaṃ ḍhaukayeccarum

vijñāpya karmaśastrāṇi pūjayettarpayedapi [kh: darpaye]

tato vijñāpya deveśa kuryādālaṃbhanaṃ punaḥ [gh: kṛtvācālaṃbhanaṃ purā]

p. 48) tāḍanaṃ bhasmanāstreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ śivavāriṇā

vastraṃ trisūtraṃ vṛkṣasya śilāyāśca kuśānvitam

saṃhitoktena mantreṇasnānavastrādikaṃ bhavet

dadyād baliṃ ca māṣānnairāmiṣāsavasaṃyutaiḥ

arghaṃ datvā ca vṛkṣasya [mū, a: rudrasya] mantra saṃśrāvyakaṃ vadet

saṅkramānyaditaḥ sthānādguṇavadyanmanoharam

yo yasmin [mū, a, k, g: yatra] vihito mantraḥ śāstre syādbalikarmaṇi

sthāne'smin yāni bhūtāni piśācā rākṣasāśca ye

tāṃstu saṃpūjya vidhivatpriyavākyaiḥ pratoṣayet

p. 49) śivārthaṃ yadbhavetkāryaṃ yuṣmākamapi tadbhavet

sukhena gacchatānyatra muktvā sthānamidaṃ drumam

śivaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivatsvapnamāṇavakaṃ [mū, a, k, kh, g: vijñāpya-] japet

svapnamāṇavakaṃ rātrau śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet

vāmāya viśvarūpāya 1 svapnādhipataye namaḥ

kriyāsiddhiṃ vidhāsyāmi tvatprasādānmaheśvara

rudreṇa cānvitaṃ svapne dṛṣṭvā vācyaṃ śubhaṃ sadā

kapyuṣṭrairbhakṣamāṇaṃ vā taṃ tyajedaśubha [k: jeccāśubha] pradam

juhuyācca tadā snātvāghoraṃ doṣanivṛttaye

aindre saumye tatheśāne avyaktādimukhaṃ kramāt

kṣaudrājyābhyaktaśastreṇa ghorāstreṇābhimantrya ca

ācāryaḥ prathamaṃ hanyācchivāstraṃ saṃjapaṃstataḥ

vārkṣa [g: karka] mantrāntadikkaṃ (?) [g: didaṃ] vai ghaṭayelliṅgamādarāt [k, g: tadanukramāt]

saṃchindyātpatitaṃ [mū, a: saṃcintyāt-] vṛkṣamajātenaiva [k, c: jātenaiva; g: mathatenaiva] bhaktimān

gṛhṇīyāddāru liṅgārthaṃ tyajedāpāṇḍaraṃ laghu

sthāpitaṃ viparītaṃ tu rāṣṭravibhramamādiśet

adhaḥ śiro yadā liṅgaṃ tadā gotrakṣayo bhavet

tato digdevatāśāntiṃ kuryāddoṣavimuktaye

anyadikpātadoṣeṇa mucyate śāntimāpnuyāt

dadyātpūrṇāhutiṃ cāpi tataḥ kāmānavāpnuyāt

patane madhyabhaṅgaḥ syāttadā homo vidhīyate

dadyātpūrṇāhutiṃ cāpi śivenāśivahāriṇā

śilādāne tathā pūjā dārvādāne yathoditā

dvijādivarṇabhedena rūpabhedena ca sthitāḥ

yuvatī sā śilā grāhyā triṣu liṅgeṣu netarāḥ

vṛddhiṃ dadyācchilā nūnaṃ taruṇī lakṣaṇānvitā

trayo'ṅkurā diśastisro gandhāsvādau [mū: gandhasvādau] caturvirdhā

tāruṇamekamityevaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāni tu

snigdhānimnā śilā dhanyā siktā puṇyanadījalaiḥ

sugandhī niviḍāṅgī ca dramacchāyāsthitā sadā

dṛḍhā manoramā gurvī madhuradhvanibhūṣitā

krameṇābjājyagovarvo [mū, a, gh: gocarvī] mūtragandhāḥ śubhāḥśilāḥ

śāntiṃ jayaṃ dhanaṃ cāyuścāturvarṇye prayacchati

sutejā rūpasaṃpannā dhanadeśendra [kh: śendru] dik sthitā

nakṣatraṃ ca mṛgaṃ tasyāstaddevaḥ śaśalāñchanaḥ

asyā ṛkṣaṃ bhavejjyeṣṭhā devatā ca śatakratu

p. 53) nakṣatraṃ phalgunī cāsyā devaśca [mū: devatā] bhagasaṃjñakaḥ

nakṣatramaśvinau [mū, a: maśvinī] cāsyā devau caivāśvinau [gh: dvau cāśvinau] matau [mū: smṛtau]

śītalā ca sukhasparśā niviḍāṅgī manoramā

ekavarṇaḥsthiraḥsnigdho gajakaṇṭho [kh: ghaṇṭo] pamasvanaḥ

pāṣāṇo'ya guru pumān puruṣāṇāṃvibhūtidaḥ

striyā strīpratimā kāryā ṣaṇḍhaṃ ṣaṇḍhena nā nṛbhiḥ

śirāḍhyaṃ karkaśaṃ tva rju [k: varja; kh: vakṣya; g: tvajjā] idamaśma napuṃsakam

śeṣāṇāṃ strīśilāḥ proktāḥ pīṭhikādyāśca [g: yāśca] śūlinaḥ

sarvavarṇātmake śvetāḥśeṣāśca śubhadāḥ [mū, a, k, kh, g, ṅ: stvaśubhadā] smṛtāḥ

p. 54) raktā pītā ca bhūpasya viśaḥ śyāmakulatthabhāḥ

indracāpopamārekhāḥ sarvāśrīvasubhūtidāḥ

lakṣaṇāḍhyālpadoṣā yā grāhyā sā homaśodhitā [kh: śobhitā]

indreśānendukāṣṭhāsu tasyā mastakamiṣyate

trividheṣvapi liṅgeṣu śilāyāṃ nāparo vidhiḥ

doṣān vṛddhādikān brūmaścāṣṭāśītiprabhedataḥ

śūṣiraṃ sphoṭakaṃ [kh, gh: sphāṭikaṃ] vyomaṃ gārā [kh: galā] paṭalajālakam

lakṣaṇaṃ kathayāmyeṣāṃ saṃkṣepeṇa prabhedataḥ

niṣṭhurā cātirūkṣā ca vṛddhā kāryavināśinī

ṭaṅkaghātāsahiṣṇuñca na tāṃ devāya yojayet

tasmādbimbātmakaṃ cāśma saṃchindyāddāru vai tathā

saptānyāni ca citrāṇi caturdhā lepanaṃ tathā

kāpilaṃ haritālābhaṃ kāpotamalivarṇakam [kh, gh: mallikādhautavarṇakam]

kiṃśukābhaṃ kusumbhābhamatasīpuṣpasannibham

campakābhaṃ ca nīlābhaṃ miśralohitapītakam

raktaṃ [kh: rūkṣaṃ] ca pāṇḍaraṃ caiva garbhadoṣān vadāmyata [kh: myaham]

guḍavarṇe śilānyā syāttenāvṛṣṭirbhavecciram

mañjiṣṭhābhe bhavedbhekaḥ śasyanāśo jalādbhayam

pīte godhā nṛpaṃ hanyāṅṭavāṃ pīḍākarī ca sā

tālābhe karkaṭaḥpittarogadarbhikṣakṛcca saḥ

jyeṣṭhā kapotakaṇṭhābhe vyādhidāridryabhītidā

nīlake makṣikā sā ca hanyādbhāryāṃ saputrakām [kh: saputrikām]

nṛpakṣī cāṣavarṇe syāttena kartuḥprajākṣayaḥ

kiṃśuke śakragopaḥ syāttena rājño dhanakṣayaḥ

atasīpuṣpasaṃkāśe garbhe vidyāt pipīlikā

ityaṣṭādaśa bimbāni kathitānyatra saṃkhyayā

bhaveyuḥ kṛmayo garbhe nāśo dehārthayostataḥ

tena rājā ca mantrī ca kārako mriyate kramāt

raktacitre ca śṛṅgī syāttayā śokabhayaṃ muhuḥ [gh: śokakaraṃ bahu]

kṛṣṇacitre ca gonāśaḥ so'nekavyādhikāraka

saptaitāni tu citrāṇi jñeyāni bāhyalakṣaṇaiḥ

caturvikārabhedena garbhaṃ vidyātpralepataḥ

saurāṣṭrī tālasaṃyuktaṃ [kh: kalasaṃyuktaiḥ] sauvīreṇa [mū, a: saurīreṇa] ca peṣayet [gh: pāvayet]

bhṛṅgāhī kṛṣṇamatsyaṃ vā jñātvā tā doṣadāstyajet

piṣṭavaivaṃ samabhāgaṃ tu nārī kṣīreṇa lepayet

tena karturbhavenmṛtyustasmāttāṃ salile kṣipet

ahorātroṣite raktaṃ tyājyamūrmānvitaṃ ca yat [kh: kurmānvitaṃ yataḥ]

tīkṣṇaṃ rudrajaṭāyuktamavikṣīraiḥ prapeṣya ca

dvayordvirūpakaṃ garbhe jñātvā prāgdoṣadāṃstyajet

kartustatkulahṛnnūnaṃ gurordadyātkṣayāmayam

ṭaṅkāghātādbhidākārī [kh: tādbhayā; mū: tābhidhā] sandhiḥ kartṛvināśakaḥ [mū, a: karturvināśakṛt]

sphoṭaṃ [gh: sphāṭaṃ] sphuṭitakaṃ vidyācchastraghātāntadāyakam

vyomākāro bhavedvyoma so'cirātkartṛnāśakaḥ

tayā kartṛprajānāśaḥ śīghrameva na saṃśayaḥ

tena kuryānna liṅgāni kṛte ca dvau vinaśyataḥ

pītaṃ sitaṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ raktaṃ nīlaṃ ca miśrakam

navatyardhaṃ mahādoṣā bhedabhinno iti smṛtāḥ

p. 59) brahmopendravibhāgasthāṃstridhā liṅgeṣvadoṣadān [mū: liṅgeṣu doṣadān]

śirā [śirāśabdo'yaṃ tālavyo dantyaścetyūṣmabhede] granthituṣāraiśca vimalaṃ dadu bindubhiḥ

viḍālāśvoṣṭragomāyukākapādāṅkitāṃstyajet

granthirūponnatagranthirjaṭharāmayadāyaka

tuṣāraṃ tadvijānīyā putraśokārtidāyakam [kh, gh: kārakam]

hemaghoṣāmayaṃ tāmra tadduṣṭaṃ piṅgalāmate

janaṃ hantyāyasākrāntā śilā tāmī ca [gh: tāmrācca] śilpinam

kṛṣṇaḥ pīto'ruṇo binduḥ sa śokabhayarogadaḥ

pīte dhanakṣayaḥ kartuḥ kṛṣṇe bandho dvayordvidhā

rekhā rekhānubiddhā vā [mū, a, k: ca] pārthivaṃ sā vināśayet

svāmidhnī ca catuśchinnā bahucchinnā kulāntakṛt

makṣikā makṣikākārā tayā kuṣṭhaṃ sudāruṇam

tadā nānā gadān kuryāt kartuścaiva na saṃśayaḥ

tyājyaṃ tadā tridhā liṅgaṃ nṛparāṣṭraprajārthinā

vāmapārśvāśritaḥ kiṃciddhanyādrāṣṭraṃ sarājakam

sarvavarṇā bhṛśaṃ [kh, gh: daśa] nūnaṃ prāṇahṛtkṛṣṇahīrikā

unnato lakṣyate yaśca [mū: yatra] viḍālāṅghriḥ sa doṣabhāk [kh: kṛt]

śilāyā rūpamālakṣya tyajettāmaśubhapradām

svalpadoṣān prakṣyāmi yathā śāstreṣu bhāṣitān

valmīkoṣaracityutthā koṣṇā kṣārāmbumadhyagā

kudiksthā sūryasantaptā kadutthāmethyadūṣitā [k, g: bhūṣitā]

duṣṭajīvasvarā yā ca yojitā nānyakarmaṇi

yā yopalānulagnā sā snehavicchittikārikāḥ [mū, a: kāraṇam]

ūṣarasthārthahṛjjñeyā citisthā duḥkhadāyinī

pāṣadāpuṇyadeśasthā vadhakṛdbhilla [kh: liṅga] sevitā

dāhaṃ hāniṃ ca śokaṃ ca naiḥsvaṃ rogaṃ tathāvahet

surāpakṣivasāgandhā udvegajananī śilā

vajradagdhāgnidagdhā ca rājño'nalabhayapradā

anyakarmārthamānītonmādāniṣpattikāriṇī

p. 62) antyajā sā śilā jñeyā dvijādīnāṃ vigarhitā [gh: tu garhitā]

nāna varṇā ca cāṇḍalī sā kartuḥ [kh: māna] kulanāśinī

prākṛtānāṃ hitārthāya kāmaṃ muktyarthināṃ nṛṇām

svalpadoṣā ime khyātā viṃśatirlakṣabhedataḥ

([copāntargato'yaṃ, k, kh, g pustakeṣu nāsti] jñaya sadbhūmibhūtasya pāṣāṇasya drumasya ca

tajja tīśena mantreṇa tajjātijananaṃ bhavet

lāñcha yatvā yathānyāyaṃ kartayecchodhayedapi

piṇḍikāsahitaṃ tacca viṣṇubhāgāntamunnatam

ekakhaṇḍaiva kartavyā piṇḍikā vai śivasya tu

praṇālamaindrasaumye vā jalādhāramadhastathā

na tu brahmaśilā prājñairiti śāstreṣu [k, g: tantreṣu] bhāṣitam

liṅgachedordhvabhāgasthā tathādho [mū, a, k: tadadho] bhāgagāpi vā

ucchrāyena tu liṅgānāmekakhaṇḍo vidhīyate

anyeṣāmapi liṅgānāṃ krameṇaikāṅgulādhilā [gh: -ṇaiva-]

([cāpāntargatoyaṃ pāṭhaḥ k, g pustakayornāsti] svamānānmekhalā tasyāḥ khātaṃ vā tatpramāṇataḥ

brahmāṃśādadhikaḥ [gh: brahmāṅgā-] khāto yavai ṣaḍbhiḥ pravistaraḥ

ratnādhārāṇi tanmadhye vṛttāni nava [mū: na ca] kalpayet

svamānavistarārdhena bāhulyena samanvitā

vārkṣī brahmaśilā vārkṣe liṅge syātpiṇḍikā tathā

arcayedastra [gh: cchatra] puṣpādyairānetuṃ mantravittamaḥ

tāpasāñchivabhaktāṃśca tarpayitvānayedratham

rathe kṛtvā ca deveśaṃ pūjayitvā tamānayet

yadā rathe rathāṅgānāṃ [mū: ratharathāṅgānāṃ] pramādātskhalitaṃ bhavet

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hutvā mucyate sarvaduṣkṛtaiḥ

rathaṃ mārgagataṃ kṛtvā śanaiḥ svapuramānayet

śobhāṃ dhvajapatākādyaiḥ strībhirmaṅgalagītakaiḥ

p. 65) prāsādasyottare pārśve karmakuryānnivedayet

pañcasaptaikādaśabhiḥ karaiḥ karmakuṭī matā

prāsādottaradiggarte kṣiptvā tāṃ pūrayenmṛdā

tarpayedastrarājaṃ ca mahāpāśupataṃ ca yat

evaṃ santarpite hyastre [k, g: jyeṣṭhe] digdoṣairmucyate śilā

tasya liṅgasya sadvipraḥ kanyā śilpī ca pūjitā

dhruvaṃ kartavyamityāha bhagavān parameśvaraḥ

śilāṃ tāṃ varjayedyatnānnaśastā liṅgakarmaṇi

evaṃ dārumayaṃ cāpi [mū, a, k, kh: vāpi] liṅgādidravyamānayet

prāguktareśakāṣṭhāsu śilāṃ nyasya kvacinmate

athavā mṛtyujetāraṃ vidyāṅgaiḥ [g: vidyāhvaiḥ] ṣaḍbhiranvitam

ityanena vidhānena gṛhītvā dāru vāśma [k, kh: raśma] vā

liṅgaṃ gurvājñyā kuryāttridhā mānopalakṣitam

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye dāruśilāgrahaṇaṃ nāma tṛtīyo vidhiḥ

caturaṅgulamānena [mū, a, kh, g, gh, ṅ: calamaṅgula-] dvāragarbhakaraiḥ sthitam

keśāgralikṣāyūkābhyāṃ [mū: yūkānāṃ] kramādaṣṭāṣṭabhiryavaḥ

p. 67) tiryak samodaraiḥ proktaṃ mānārthaṃ pārameśvare

kalāgolakanetrāṅka [gh: netrāhva] mātrābhirdvyaṅgalaṃ smṛtam

ardhahastaṃ vitastiṃ ca tālaṃ ca dvādaśāṅgulam

liṅgadhāmāsanādīnāṃ sarvakarmavidhau matā

dvāramānāttrisaṃkhyākaṃ navadhā garbhamānataḥ

evaṃ liṅgāni ṣaṭtriṃśajjñeyāni jyeṣṭhamānataḥ

eṣāmaikyena liṅgānāṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ bhavet

yāni yāni ca nāmāni tāni tāni pṛthagbruve [kh: bhavet]

sparśaśabdeśvaro nāsā jihvākṣitvakśrutīśvarāḥ

vacanagrahaṇotsargānandapragamaneśvarāḥ

p. 68) prakṛtīśaḥ pumāṃścaiva madhyānāṃ daśa pañca ca

vidyeśvaraḥ patiḥ sraṣṭā garutmāna sakalo'kalaḥ

śāntaścājo [mū: tejo] mahātejā dvārmānāduttamaṃ trayam

skandaḥ sūkṣmo' [mū: skandhaḥ sūryo] mbikānāthastrayaṃ dvārāttathādhamam

bhujaṅgī vṛṣabhī siṃhī uttamā nava garbhajāḥ

vyāpī śūlī viśālī [g: kirātī] cetthadhamā nava garbhajāḥ [gh: garbhikāḥ]

sarvajño'tha vṛṣadhvajo niyāmaḥ pāpahā [gh: niryāmaḥ pāśahā] dhruvaḥ

śambhuḥ sthāṇurharo ḍiṇḍī hartā kartā śivo'vyayaḥ

bhadro'tha vimalo jyeṣṭho gāṇḍīvaḥ puṣṭivardhanaḥ

navaite hastamānena tvadhamāḥ parikīrtitāḥ

ṣaḍādidaśaparyantaṃ calaliṅgaṃ ca madhyamam

śreṣṭhaliṅgapramāṇaṃ tajjyeṣṭhādyaṅgulilakṣitam

ravibhirhematārotthaṃ liṅgaṃ śeṣai [mū, a, k: śeṣairanyai] stripañcabhiḥ

ārogyaṃ rājyasaukhyaṃ ca nṛpaśrījayalokakṛt

calaliṅgādbhavetkāryaṃ sādhakānāṃ kvacinmate [kh: tsamā]

dvātriṃśatṣoḍaśāṃśāśca koṇayostu vilopayet

pārśvābhyāṃ tu viluptābhyāṃ calaliṅgaṃ bhavedvaram [mū, a: ddhru vam]

p. 70) liṅgaṃ dvārocchrayādardhādbhavetpādonataḥ kramāt [mū, a, k, g, ṅ: samāt]

tayormadhye ca sūtrāṇi sapta saṃpādayetsamam

dvyantare rāmarāmaṃ ca liṅgānāṃ dīrghatā nava

strīya?madhyottamaṃ liṅgaṃ trividhaṃ [k, kh: tridhaiva] trividhātmakam

liṅgāni ghaṭayeddhīmān ṣaṭsu vāṣṭāntareṣu vā

tadekaṃ sthāpayelliṅgaṃ hastātpādajameva vā

gabhādyaśo'rthaputrastrībhūrjayo'rtho ghṛṣāyuṣī

siddhī rājyaṃ karaiḥ kāryā liṅgādantajāttathā

dhvajādyā yaiḥ svarairbhūtaiḥ śikhibhirvā haretkṛtī

p. 71) dhvajādiṣu dhvajaṃ siṃhaṃ vṛṣākhyaṃ ca gajaṃ śubham

dhvaje rājyaṃ jayaṃ siṃhe vṛṣe lābhaṃ śriyaṃ gaje

dhvāṅkṣeṇa sarvanāśaḥ syāttasmāttān parivarjayet

lakṣmīprado bhavet ṣaḍjo gāndhāro janavṛddhikṛt

ṛṣabheṇārivṛddhiḥ syānmadhyamaḥ sutanāśakṛt [mū, a, k, g, ṅ: śubhanāśanaḥ]

bhūteṣu kṣmācarāścānye toyādyāścāśubhā [gh: toyāḍhyā] bhṛśam

sajvaro [k, g: saddhīro] dāhakṛttejo vāyustu kulanāśakṛt

śasto'gniścāhavanīyo dvāvagnī cāśubhau matau

karturmṛtyuprado hyagnistṛtīyo nṛpabhītidaḥ

ṣaḍvā tadāyabhāgaṃ tu kṛtvaikaṃ saṃkṣipedvaram

bhāgeśa cāpyanīśaṃ ca devejyaṃ tulyasaṃjñakam

dairghyaṃ māyānvitaṃ kṛtvā liṅgaṃ kuryāttrirūpakam

uktāyāmasya cārdhāṃśe [mū: cāyāṃśe] nāgāṃśai [k, kh: bhāgārdhaiḥ] rbhāṃjite kramāt

ādyānādyasurejyāhva [mū: -jyāṅka] tulyānāṃ caturasratā

dvidhā bhedādbahūnyatra vakṣyante [k, g: lakṣyante] viśvakamataḥ

tridhā hastājjivākhyaṃ ca yuktaṃ sarvasamena ca

pañca [mū: yaśca] saptatirekatvājjinaibhaktaṃbhavanti hi

evamaṅgulavṛddhyā ca navaikakaragarbhataḥ

vistāraṃ saptadhā kṛtvā sthāpyaṃ vā madhyatastrayam

ekaikāṃśaṃ caturbhāgaṃ koṇahrāsaṃ dvibhāgataḥ

tadekaikaṃ yugaṃ kṛtvā ekaikāṃśaṃ samāharet

kvacitkaṇaṃpraveśena lāñchayitvāsiśātanam

tanmadhye sūtrasambhrānte rudro bhavati vartulaḥ

kṣipedvā śūnyadoṣaghnaṃ hastādekāṅgulaṃ kramāt

ṣoḍaśāṃśa kṣipedādye [kh, g, gh: nnādye] vedāṃśamamarorcite [kh: samākhyatrisamaiḥ surāḥ]

piṅgalāyā mate proktamādyāmarārcitena tu

p. 74) bhaveyurvardhamānāni vasudviṣaṭ [gh: vasvatviṣaṭ] śatāni ca

viśvakarmamataṃ [gh: mate] dṛṣṭvā vaktavyaṃ pīṭhavartanam [gh: pīṭhavartane]

tacca savasamaṃ liṅgaṃ prāsāde vā gṛhe hitam

dvau pādau jānuparyantaṃ caturasrepitāmahaḥ

āmūrdhaṃ nābhitaścordhvaṃ vṛttabhāge haraḥ svayam

pañcadhāvyaktaliṅgāni samapīṭhāni tāni ca

pañcaliṅgavyavastheyaṃ śirovartinyathocyate [mū, a: vartanamucyate; k, kh: vartanyathocyateḥ]

ekaikasya caturbhedaiḥ kāmyarūpātphala vadet

p. 75) cirarājyaprada khyātaṃ chatrabhedāścaturvidhāḥ

mānada [mū: mānāṅga] pakṣa [k, kh, g: padma] lopācca vipulaṃ khyātidaṃ bhavet

śrīvāsaṃ putradaṃ kurturdakṣāṇḍaṃ syāccaturvidham

puṣṭidaṃ dvikṣayātpūrṇaṃ mahāhlādakarasadā [kh: tathā]

āyudaṃ kumudaṃ jñeyaṃ candaṃ caturvidhaṃ matam [mū: jñeyaṃ candraṃ caturvidham]

sarvakāmapradaṃ proktaṃ vināśaṃ dvikṣayācchriye

ballabhatvaprada tacca caturdhā trapuṣaṃ bhavet

kuryādviprādikāryārthamekaṃ vā kukkuṭāṇḍakam [kh: ṭāṇḍabhaṃ]

dīrghāyuṣṭvamatha cendoḥ [mū, a, k, kh, g, ṅ: mathārdhendoḥ] kṛkavākvaṇḍataḥ prajñāḥ

jinaviṃśatiśārdūlairādye brahmādiveṣṭanam

vikāreṇa smareṇāṃśatṛtīyāṃśayutena ca

bhāgatṛtīyabhāgena nakhacandrānvitena ca

sasaptadaśabhiścāṃśaiḥ paridhiḥ syātsamāṃśake [mū: samāhvake]

sārdhadvitribhujairviṣṇuḥ sāṅghryekatribhujairharaḥ

yavagarbhe bhavetpuṣṭiḥ pānānnaṃ jaṭharonnate

samakukṣau bhayaṃ hanyāt sthūlamūle tvarogatā [mū: hyarogatā]

nimne ca luñcite kuryāddhanadhānyavināśanam

chidrānvitaṃ ca sadbhāyā [mū, a, k: tathā bhāryā] lalāṭe nimnaka sutam

p. 77) madhye kṛśaṃ ca śokāya dubhikṣadamadhaḥ kṛśam

vraṇitāmayavṛddhiśca durbhikṣaṃ cātikoṭare

dhanaghnaṃ pṛṣṭhanimnaṃ syādrogaṃ pārśve tathodare

samyak sunirvraṇaṃ dhautaṃ tadā cinhāni lakṣayet

makarādarśacāpobhisrakkamaṇḍalutoraṇāḥ

śrīvatsanandikāvartarathahārebhabhūṣaṇaiḥ

mayūravājivāsobhistaruvajrapavitrakāḥ

liṅgāṅge yatra tatra [gh: yatra yatra] syānnṛparāṣṭravivṛddhidāḥ

karaṅkakaṅkagṛdhrāhiśyenakākakharākhubhiḥ

yajamānaprajārājñāṃ liṅgaṃ bhīśa kadāyakam [mū: strīśoka]

ete ca pārthivā varṇā liṅgacinhāni bhūtaye [k: bhūtale]

ete ca [gh: tu] vāruṇā varṇā prajāyurdhanalābhadāḥ

āgneyā agnisantrāsadurbhikṣavyādhivardhanāḥ

vāyavyā vāyusantrāsakalahādvegakārakāḥ

sādhane cāstriliṅgaṃ ca digliṅgaṃ caiva vakṣyate [kh: lakṣyate]

tryasraṃ cāśeṣasiddhyarthaṃ vedāsraṃ cepsitapradam

saptāsraṃ dveṣakṛtkhyātamaṣṭāsraṃ kīrtivardhanam

rudrāsraṃ rogavṛddhyarthaṃ bhayaghnaṃ dvādaśāsrakam

tithyasraṃ puṇyakṛlliṅgaṃ vikārāsraṃ sukhapradam

navāvadhikarāntasya śūnyadoṣo na kasyacit

tridhā mānena caikena [gh: caikyena] ṣoḍaśāsraṃ śatadvayam

caturaṣṭaṣaḍasraṃ tu indrāṅkaṃ [kh: candrāhvaṃ] vajrarūpakam

bindusaṃpātsaṃyogāttiryaksūtrairvivartayet

vasvasrordhvaṃ tu [mū: vasvasrordhe tu] saptāsraṃ śaktyāgneya ca dāhakṛt

nāhārdhaṃ navadhā kṛtvā śarairhṛtpṛṣṭhakoṭibhiḥ

evaṃ cāṣṭāsravṛtaṃ syādyāmyadaṇḍaṃ [mū, a, k, g, ṅ: yāmadaṇḍaṃ] rujapradam

aṣṭāsro'rdha [mū: -'rdhaṃ] daśāstraṃ syātkhaḍgābhaṃ rākṣasaṃ bhayam [k, kh: kṣaye]

caturaṣṭāsravṛttaṃ syātpāśavadvāruṇaṃ śubham

kṛśaṃ prāgrūpakaṃ dīrghaṃ calamuccāṭakṛddhvajam

yākṣaṃ [k: pākṣaṃ; g: mākṣaṃ] gadāgramūrdhānaṃ balavṛddhikaraṃ [gh: dhana-] śivam

catuḥpañcāsrakaṃ [mū: caturasrāsrakaṃ] tryasraṃ śūlaṃ raudraṃ tu muktidam

aṣṭāsro' [g: aṣṭāṃśo] rdhaṃ ca vedāsraṃ brāhmaṃ svargāya padmavat

caturaṣṭāsravṛttaṃ syādvaiṣṇavaṃ śaṅkhava [mū, a: śaktivat] cchubham

rudrāttrihānitaḥ śuktirdaṇḍo digbhyastrihānitaḥ

kukkuṭāṇḍaṃ ca vāyavye bhūtāṃśāddhi yate gadā

dakṣāṇḍabhaja vaiṣṇośca ittha rekhā maṇerapi

sphuṭitaṃ garbhavaddaṣṭaṃ sthāpayedābhivārike

vyaktaliṅgāni cānyāni nānā rūpāṇi vacmyataḥ

iti [iti pratiṣṭhātantre, likhitapustakeṣu pāṭhaḥ] lakṣaṇasamuccaye'vyaktaliṅgalakṣaṇaṃ nāma [liṅgavartanāvidhiḥ, iti likhita pustakeṣu pāṭhaḥ |] caturtho vidhiḥ

pūrvāṅgulapramāṇena daśapañcāṅgulāṅgulāt

kvaciddhastapramāṇā sā kvacicca triṃśadaṅgulā

p. 82) dvārocchrayaṃ vibhajyādāvaṣṭadhaukaṃ [mū, a, k: vaṣṭadhaikaṃ] vidhāya ca

vidhātavyā dvibhāgena kanyasī pratimā ca sā

madhyamā syāddvibhāgena [gh: madhyamārcā vibhāgena] kāryaikāṃśena pīṭhikā

kartavyā taddvibhāgena pratimā sottamā matā

navāntaṃ tritrikānteṣu kanyasādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

pīṭha [gh: pīṭhaṃ] dvārapravistāraṃ mayaścakresvake mate

tithimāsanavatyardhaihīnamadhyottamāstridhā

paṅgurūpāśca [gh: paṅgurūpāḥ sa] kūṣmāṇḍāḥ saptadhārcāśca [mū: dhārvāśca]vartayet

vibhajya śaktisūtreṇa ṛkṣādibhyaḥ [mū, a, k, kh: ṛkṣyādityaḥ] śubhe dine

pīṭhātmāśrayajīvāṃśca prabhādīṃśca prakalpayet

kṛtvādhoṃśagataḥ [mū: cādhogataḥ] pīṭhaṃ tribhāge jīvakalpanā

sātmāśrayāṃ sadūtīṃ [mū: sabhūtāṃ] ca pratimāṃ vartayettataḥ

bhāgaikamaṅgula kṛtvā mahārcāṃ vatayedbudhaḥ

eka bhāge'ṅgulaṃ tasyā [gh: tasyāṃ] stenāṅgaṃ parikalpayet

maye sūtraṃ samaṃ nyasya ṛjvarcāṃ kārayecchubhām

jīvasyāddairghyamānena pratyaṅgānyadhunā vruve

mātrābhistithisaṃkhyābhirmukuṭa [mū, a, k, kh: mukuṭe] veṣṭanaṃ bhavet

tālamānaṃ bhavedvakraṃ grīvā ca caturaṅgulā

tālaikaikapramāṇaṃ syādūrū tālavinirgamau

p. 84) jñeyau gulphau dvinetrau ca pārṣṇīdvyaṅgulake mate [k: kramāt]

madhye kalādvayocchrāyovagresārdhārdha [kh: sā cārdha] mātrakau

pañcāṅgulau veṣṭanena sārdhāṅgaladviparvaṇī

tryaṅgulā pariṇāhena kathyante tvadhunāparāḥ

pādāṅgulyastriparvāṇaḥ parvārdhanakhaśobhitāḥ

kārāṅghri [mū, a, k, kh: nakhāṅghri] talakaṃ raktaṃ nakhāḥ savonnatāruṇāḥ

upabāhū daśāṣṭāṃśau ṣoḍaśāṅgulakau bhujau

bhūtāṅgulā bhavenmadhyā ardhahīnā tvanāmikā

madhyamādyāstriparvāṇo dviparvāṅguṣṭhako mataḥ

tarjanyanāmikāparva sapādayavavarjitam

dvyaṅgulo'ṅguṣṭhakagranthirāsāṃ parvārdhagā nakhāḥ

nāsotsedhaḥ savistārodvyaṅgulo dvipuṭaḥ [mū, a: dvikuṭaḥ] śubhaḥ

dvyardhaṃ yavaṃ puṭe sthūle madhye vaśa [k, kh: viṃśaḥ] stribhiryavaḥ

adhe'paro'ṅgulakaṃ tu [k, g: ṅ gulakarṇa] dvyaṅgule cibukasthitiḥ

nāsikāmūlapārśve tu locane dvyaṅgulāyate [g: locanamaṅgulāyate]

indīvarābjapatrābhā paṭolaphalavatstriyāḥ

pañcamāṃśaṃ bhavejjyotiḥ sṛkkaṇīsūtratulyataḥ

ardhāṅgulasamā droṇī sārdhadvyaṅguladīrghatā

p. 86) tayora taramātrārdhaṃ striyāṃ yavārdhavistṛte [gh: yathāmbuvistṛte]

karṇau netradayāyāmau tadardhādvistarastayoḥ

kalārdhaṃ (tadukau [mū,a: tuḍhukau; k, g: tuṭukau; kh: tupukau] khalvau ?) pāśau kuryādyathepsitau

indrasaṅkhyāṅgulaṃ paścātkarṇayorantaraṃ viduḥ

lalāṭo'ṣṭāṅgulāyāmastryaṅgulaḥ śaṅkha ucyate

saptāṅgulau kapolau tu sāṅgulau sayavocchrayau

hanū kalā tradīrghau vdikalaścibukodgamaḥ

skandho'ṣṭāṅgulavistārastadāyāmaścatuṣkalā

vistāraḥ kūrparastasmādbhūtāṅgulairyavaistathā

p. 87) caturaṅgulavistāro maṇibandhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ

stanayorantaraṃ tālaṃ tayoḥ prāntau dvinetrakau

nābhiḥ pradakṣiṇāvartā nimnato'rdhāṅguloditā

hṛdviṃśatyaṅgulaṃ proktaṃ nābhideśaścaturdaśa

pañcādaśāṅgulaṃ [mū, a, k, g: pañcāśadaṅgulaṃ] vakṣo hṛddeśaḥ pañcabhirvinā

kaṭiḥ saṃveṣṭanenoktā śatācca [k, kh: sa tāvat] dvyadhikāṅgulā

caturnetrapravistārau gajakumbhopamau śubhau

mehanastryaṅgulo dairghyātyariṇāhe [mū: pariṇāhāt] ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ

pāyoścavṛṣaṇasyāntavistāreṇa dvigolakam

tadadhaḥ piṇḍikāyāṃ ca jaṅghāyāṃ ghaṇṭikordhvataḥ

p. 88) evaṃ vistāra eteṣāṃ veṣṭanaṃ triguṇādhikam

bhujajaṅghāgrake caiva svavistārāttriveṣṭanam

nāryaṣṭāṃśā ca tanvaṅgī [mū, a, kh: tattvaṅgī] cakṣuḥ śroṇī stanādhikā

tanmadhyatastvadhobhāge randhraṃ syāt kuñjaroṣṭhavat

vṛttaṃ vedāsrakaṃ cāsyaṃ kuryātpretādirakṣasām

brahmaviṣṇuharādīnāṃ saṃkṣepāllakṣma cocyate

vakṣobāhūrujaṅghānāṃ [gh: jaṅghāyāṃ] karādīnāṃ krameṇa tu

dve dve caivāṅgule deye vaktrādiṣu yathākramam

vāmanaḥ pañcatālaśca bhūtāṃśaiḥ paṅgukubjakau

buddhvā buddhvā tu yatnena sūkṣmadṛṣṭyā vicakṣaṇaḥ

p. 89) acatanmānataḥ kāryāḥ sarvāvayavabhūṣitā

sthānakaiḥ karaṇairyuktā pratimaiṣā phalapradā

vakrāṅgī [mū, a, k, kh, ṅ: cakrāṅgī] cipiṭā dīnā kṛśa [kh: bhūṣa] gātrī ca raudrikā

saṅkocakārikā stabdhā hīnavaktrā ca rogadā

hīnajaṅghārisaukhyāthaṃ śuṣkā syānnṛpanāśinī

udvegajananī sā tu bhrāmaṇī ca sadā bhavet

vakrāṅgyarthāṅganāśāya cipiṭā duḥkhadāyinī

dhanaghnī ca [mū: tu] kṛśāṅgī syācchasyaghnī tu kṛśodarī

suhṛddhanasutāṃstasmātkāryā sallakṣaṇāḥ sadā

p. 90) chāyāgataṃ ca sarvāṅga [gh: sarvāṅka] bhittikaṃ kṣiptakaṃ tathā

karaṇāni śataṃ cāṣṭau vyantarā [k, kh: pyantarā] ścātivistarāḥ (?)

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye vyaktaliṅgalakṣaṇaṃ nāma pañcamo vidhiḥ

kapālābhayahastogrā vāmādyā navaśaktayaḥ

dvīpicarmāhibhṛcchāntastryakṣo jaṭendubhūṣitaḥ

aṅgaṣaṭkasamāyuktaḥ śīghraṃ syātsiddhidāyakaḥ

[atra ka. pustakasyā ṭippaṇī-yuva= īśaḥ, yauvanaḥ=puruṣaḥ, vṛddhaḥ=aghora, strī=vāmadevaḥ, bālaḥ=sadyojātaḥ, iti |] yuvayauvanavṛddhastrībālābhaḥ pañcavaktrabhṛt

vāmenā [kh: dakṣeṇa; atraiva k. pustakasya ṭippaṇyāṃ akṣamālā iti śabdaśva prakṣiptaḥ] bhayamantrāṅka [gh: mantrāhva] kapālotpalapūra [atrāpi k. pustakasya ṭippaṇyāṃ bījapura iti prakṣiptaḥ] bhṛt

dṛgraktāsihutāśābhāḥ sarve tryakṣā jaṭānvitāḥ

babhrubhrūśmaśruddā?bhīmamastraṃ pītaṃ svaśastra [k, kh, g: suśastra] bhṛt

śakti [pā: śānti] vajrākṣamālāsicakrāṇi dakṣiṇe kvacit

ekāsyaḥ pañcavaktro vā tryakṣo jaṭendubhṛcchivaḥ

p. 92) koṇeṣu caika [mū: caiva] rudrādyāḥ sitāsaṅnīlalohitāḥ

śivāyudhadharāstryakṣāḥ sṛṇyasibhyāṃ vinā kvacit

puṭāñjalikarāḥ sarve vidyeśāścaikavaktrakāḥ

varābhayakapālābjajaṭendvajinanāgabhṛt

dakṣiṇe śūlanistriśakuṭhārābhayadhārakaḥ

muṇḍamālī śavārūḍho dvaitādvaite kramānmataḥ

saumyaṃ prahasitāsyaṃ ca candramauliṃ jaṭādharam

nāgendramekhalāyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam [kh: maṇḍitam]

nṛtyantaṃ mahāhṛṣṭamāyudhākula [mū, a: māyudhojjvala] pāṇinam

balanākārakaṃ bāhumekaṃ kṛtvā hṛdi sthitam

vāme pratānitabhujaṃ samyak kṛtvā suśobhanam

([koṣṭhāntargato'yaṃ pāṭhaḥ k. pustake nāsti] sarvaśāntikaraṃ kuryādityevaṃ saumyarūpakam

skandanandimahākālagaṇeśagaṇasaṃyutam

sānandai rūpasampannairvīkṣyamāṇaṃ maheśvaram [kh, gh: vīkṣamāṇairmaheśvara]

tadagre ca pranṛtyaṃśca bhṛṅgīkāryaṃ kṛśo bhṛśaḥ

vikṛtāstīkṣṇanāsāścacaurāgnyādipradāḥ [kh: prapadāḥ] kramāt

harasyaivaṃ bhavedarcā sthāne sthāne phalapradā

bhrukuṭībhīṣaṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ lelihānaṃ ca draṃṣṭriṇam

nāgopavītinaṃ dīptaṃ nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam

caturdaśakaraikāsyaṃ saumyaṃ vyāghrājināmbaram

khaṭvāṅgaṃ kheṭakaṃ pāśaṃ cāpābhayakapālakam

mātṛbhūtānvitaṃ kuryādbhairavaṃ śāntikārakam

kuryādaghoraghaṇṭeśaṃ dviraṣṭabhujamaṇḍitam

upaviṣṭaṃ sadā kṛṣṇaṃ sarvakāmapradāyakam

p. 95) aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ kuryādvaradābhaya [g: bhavadābhaya] varjitam

ghṇṭāṃ ḍamarukaṃ caiva kuṭhāraṃ mudgaraṃ [k, g: mudgalaṃ] tathā

sarvaśatruharaṃ nityaṃ mātṛbhiḥ parivāritam

vyāghrājināṃśuka saumyaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam

yāmye saumye ca bibhrāṇa dvābhyāṃ vyākhyānakaṃ tathā

gauraṃ sitāmbaraṃ śāntaṃ kamaṇḍalusamanvitam

amṛtāgamahastaṃ ca kuryāttatpārśvayoḥ sadā

ardhacandrāsanāsīnaṃ śvetarājīvamadhyagam

p. 96) divyasiṃhāsanāsīnaṃ brahmādyaiḥ parivāritam

kapālamālinaṃ śvetaṃ yogāsanaṃ kajasthitam

triśūlaṃ cākṣasūtraṃ ca [kh, gh: tu] bibhrāṇaṃ dakṣiṇe kare

kuryānmṛtyuñjayaṃ devaṃ mṛtyurogavināśakam [mū, a, g: -śanam]

vāmāṅge lalanā kāryā yoṣidābharaṇānvitā

īśe śūlaṃ kapālaṃ ca vāme jāpyamathotpalam

kuṇḍalālaṅkṛtaḥ saumyo dvibhujo dvipicarmabhṛt

mithaḥ saṃvīkṣyamāṇāsyo yāmyahastasthitotpalaḥ

p. 97) vidheyaḥ śāstravidvadbhirūrdhvaliṅga umeśvaraḥ

haraśca bahurūpaśca tryambakaśca sureśvaraḥ

sadyaḥ prabhṛtijau dvau dvau tadvatkuḍyabjasaṃyutāḥ

vṛṣasthā varadāḥ kuṇḍīśūlapaṅkajapāṇayaḥ

śaṅkhacakrabhṛtaṃ vāme śeṣe śāntigadābhṛtam [gh: karam]

śrīvatsakaustubhoraskaṃ pīnāṅgaṃ pṛthuvakṣasam

prasannavadanaṃ saumyaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam

śaṅkhacakradhare kārye [gh: dharau kāryau] tatsaumyāgneyapārśvataḥ

p. 98) vāme'bhayapradaṃ śeṣe śaṅkhahastaṃ dvibhāhukam

kruddha udyatahastaśca prāsādasthaḥ sadā mataḥ

sandaṣṭauṣṭhapuṭaḥ kruddho viṣṇuraṣṭabhujo mataḥ

cakraṃ cāpaṃ ca khetaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ vāmakare tathā

ekāsyaścaturāsyaśca trimukho vā maye mate

tattryaṃśavistaraṃ caiva puccho'dhaṃ saptabhāgaśaḥ

hastau kroḍasamīpasthau pādau jaṭharapārśvataḥ

ardhāṅgulaṃ tu tannetraṃ dvisārdhaikārdhabhāgataḥ

śeṣaṃ rohitavatkuryānmatsyarūpadhare vibhau [k, kh, g, gh, ṅ: harervidhau]

p. 99) taddvyaṃśamadhyato vṛttaṃ kalāhīnaṃ tu pārśvayoḥ

dviguṇaṃ pādato dairghyādgrīvāṃ mastakasaṃyutām

nāsākṣinakhakarṇādi jñātvā kūrmaṃ vivartayet

hanū tadvaktrabhāge ca dairghyātsaptāṅgulau matau

daṃṣṭre ca tryaṅgule vaktre sitarṣikalikopame

prāṅmānādyavahīnaṃ tu cakragāruṇalocanam [gh: locane]

gadācakrābjaśaṅkhāḍhyamālīḍhakaraṇe sthitam

evaṃ varāharūpaṃ syādvanamālāvibhūṣitam

narāṅgaṃ [gh: varāṅgaṃ] siṃhavaktraṃ ca vṛttākṣaṃ bhrukuṭīyutam [mū, a, k, g, gh, ṅ: mukham]

tadvakṣo dārayantaṃ ca sphuraccakragadādharam

vanamālānvitaṃ kuryānnarasiṃhaṃ jagatprabhum

jaṭilaṃ kuṇḍikāyuktaṃ gauraṃ śyāmāruṇekṣaṇam

vāmāṅghryākrāntaviśvaṃ ca tadbhedaṃ tu [mū, a, k: ca] trivikramam

śyāmavarṇo bṛhadvakṣo rāmaḥ kṣatrāntakārakaḥ

jaṭī cāpeṣuhastaśca rāmaḥ kāryo daśāsyahṛt

gadālāṅgaladhārī ca rāmaḥ kaṃsāntakārakaḥ

ūrdhvapadmasthito buddho varadābhayakārakaḥ

gauro vājisthitaḥ khaḍgī kvacitpiṅgalalocanaḥ

rāmo rāmaśca rāmaśca buddhaḥ kalkī ca te daśa

ūrdhvādhaḥ karayoḥ śaṅkhapadme dakṣiṇato mate [k, kh: dakṣiṇabhāgataḥ]

jvaladagnisamākāraṃ kuryātkeśavarūpakam

vāmayośca gadācakre nīlaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ vapuḥ

savyayoḥ kambupadme ca mādhave rukmasannibhe

ūrdhvādhobjaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ ca govinde piṅgalātmake [kh: piṅgalāṅgake]

vāmadakṣiṇayoḥ kuryātsite viṣṇau kvacinmate

vāmayośca gadāpadme candrābhe madhusūdane

cakramūrdhvamadhaḥ śaṅkhaṃ gaure pūrvatrivikrame

gadābje śeṣayoḥ pīte vāmane pūrvarūpake

p. 103) gadāśaṅkhaṃ tathā vāme śrīdhare vidrumopame

suvarṇābhe hṛṣīkeśe pāṇyoḥ savyāpasavyayoḥ

gadādhaścakramūrdhvaṃ ca padmanābhe sitaprabhe

dakṣiṇe cottare haste deve dāmodare'ruṇe

pradyumnāccāniruddhācca vijñātavyāstrayastrayaḥ

mārgādikārtikāntaṃ ca proktā dvādaśamūrtayaḥ

yogamudrāṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ ca gadācakradharaṃ dvayam

mudrāṃ kuryāddhṛdisthāṃ ca aśeṣāghapraṇāśinīm

pātraśāntidharaṃ yāmye vāmake padmasīriṇau

kaumodyā hetirājena pāñcajanyāmbujena ca

nārāyaṇādipañcānyatpūrvoktakaraśastrabhṛt

ebhiryuktaṃ navavyūhaṃ jñātavyaṃ daiśikottamaiḥ

vāmotsaṅgadhṛtaśrīkamardhacandrāsane sthitam

taddakṣiṇaṃ karaṃ skandhe devadevasya dakṣiṇe

padmastho ghūrṇitākṣaśca gātṛnartanakairvṛtaḥ

advaitena vidhānena pūjanīyo vipaścitā

p. 105) yoganidrāparāvasthaḥ [kh: dharāvasthaḥ] supto'nantatanūpari

pādalagnā dharādevī jalaśāyī grahāvṛtaḥ

ābhyutthapaṅkajārūḍhaḥ pitāmahasamanvitaḥ

sā līḍho nandane rāgī sadā cāpeṣubhṛcchubhaḥ [gh: bhṛtsutaḥ]

tuṅgaghrāṇo [k, kh: tuṅgaghoṇā] narāṅghirvā pakṣijaṅgho narāṅgakaḥ

vāmajānugato bhūmau tārkṣyaḥ pakṣasamanvitaḥ

halaśaṅkhavaro mālī viśvakseno harergaṇaḥ

padmasīradharaṃ vāme śeṣe muśalagodvaham [k, kh: khādyayugodvaham]

ardhe'rdhe bhūṣaṇaiḥ svaiḥ svairyuktamūrdhvaṃ kajasthitam

akṣamālāṃ triśūlaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ ca kaumudīṃ kramāt

puṃstrīsvarūpakaṃ kuryācchivanārāyaṇātmakam

brahmāṇaṃ vratinaṃ kuryājjaṭāmukuṭabhūṣitam [kh: bhūṣaṇam]

kṛṣṇājinaṃ ca tatskandhe vastraṃ cājinavāsasam

sruvaṃ [gh: srucaṃ] kamaṇḍaluṃ vāme sakuśājyaghaṭaṃ tathā

gāyatrīṃ caiva sāvitrīmṛṣīṃstatpārśvayostathā

riktavakṣo'kṣikukṣīṃśca sākṣamālākamaṇḍalūn

kruddhāmūrdhvasthitāṃ cāru kuryādviṃśatibāhukām

ḍamaruṃ śastrikāṃ yāmye haste sa bibhratīṃ sadā

ghaṇṭādhvajagadādarśamudgarān vāmahastakaiḥ

kāryā daśabhujā cāpi saiva devī kvacinmate

śastrodyatakaraḥ kruddhastad pīvāsambhavaḥ pumān

siṃhena khādyamānaśca pāśabaddho gale bhṛśam

caṇḍikodyataśastreyamaśeṣaripunāśanī

ādau madhye tathendrādau navatattvākṣaraiḥ kramāt

p. 108) mūrdhajaṃ kheṭakaṃ ghaṇṭāmādarśaṃ tarjanīṃ dhanuḥ

śaktimudgaraśūlaṃ ca vajraṃ khaḍgamathāṅkuśam

śeṣāḥ ṣoḍaśahastāśca añjanī [mū: añjalī] ḍamaruṃ vinā

caṇḍā caṇḍavatī caiva caṇḍarūpāticaṇḍikā

rocanābhāruṇā kṛṣṇā nīlā śuklā ca dhūmrikā [kh: sūkṣmā ca dhūmikā]

mahiṣotthaḥ pumāñśastrī tatkacagrahamuṣṭikāḥ

navadurgāḥ pratiṣṭhāpya putrārthāyuravāpnuyāt

bibhratī daṇḍikākuṇḍyāvakṣasūtraṃ varaṃ kramāt

saiva rambhāvane siddhāgnihīnā lalitānyathā

yāmye phalāñjanīhastā saubhāgyā tatra cordhvikā [mū, a: cordhikā]

trinetrā jaṭilā saumyā nānābharaṇabhūṣitā

abhayaṃ kuṇḍikāṃ śūlaṃ sthitaṃ vāmakare sadā

vināvarā bhayābhyāṃ tu kartavyā savamaṅgalā

śayyārūḍhā prasuptā ca yoganidrā samāśritā

vaṃśotthakarṇikākarṇā tailābhyaktaśarīriṇī

navatyūrdhva [mū, a, k, kh: vardhanyūrdhva] vajā kṛṣṇā kālarātrī bhayaṅkarī

hemavarṇābjasaṃsthā ca pīnonnatapayodharā

sadā yāmyakarāmbhojā vāme śrīḥ śrīphalānvitā

gajau bhṛṅgārahastau ca snāpayantau ca [gh: yantyau tu] pārśvataḥ

pustā [kh: suptā] kṣamālikāhastā vīṇābhṛdvā sarasvatī

kumbhendīvarahastā ca dvārasthā janhuputrikā

indīvaradalaśyāmā surūpā yamunā tathā

dvīpicarmadharaṃ kuryādvīṇāhastaṃ ca tumburum

anantaraṃ tu mātṛśca brahmāṇyādyāstu sapta ca

kuṇḍyājyapātriṇīṃ vāme brahmāṇīṃ haṃsasaṃsthitam

kapālamālinīṃ śuklāṃ rudrāṇīṃ vṛṣasaṃsthitām [k, kh, g: vṛṣabhasthitām]

śikhipṛṣṭhasamārūḍhāṃ kaumārīṃ skandarūpiṇīm

śyāmāṃ surūpiṇīṃ kuryādvaiṣṇavīṃ vanamālinīm

suvastrāṃ yauvanodbhinnāṃ nāryābharaṇabhūṣitām

khaḍgakheṭakasaṃyuktāmathavāpi caturbhujām

indrāṇīṃ sarvasiddhyarthaṃ vastrālaṅkārasaṃyutām

vivatāsyāṃ tu daṃṣṭrogrāṃ śave vā kauśike sthitām

kapālaṃ katarīṃ yāmye pāśaṃ śūlaṃ ca vāmake

mātṛsthāne'thavaikāṃ vā tadante ca gaṇeśvaraḥ

gajacarmabhṛdūrdhvābhyāṃ ṣaḍdoḥ syādrudracarcikā [citrapustake mahālakṣmī vidyate rudracarcikaiva samucita iva pāṭhaḥ | 78 citraṃ draṣṭavyam |]

tena sā rudracāmuṇḍā [rudracāmuṇḍāyāḥ 79 saṃ0 citraṃ dra0 |] nāṭye [nāṭyeśvaryāḥ 80 saṃ ci0 dra0 |]śvaryatha nṛtyatī

nṛpājimahiṣebhāṃśca khādantī cet kare sthitān

ekāsyā śvetavarṇa ca śvetābharaṇabhūṣitā

daśabāhu [kh: śatābāhu] strinetrā ca śastrāsiṃ ḍamaruṃ nṛkam

khaṭvāṅgaṃ ca triśūlaṃ ca siddhacāmuṇḍikāhvayā

etadrūpā bhavedanyā pāśāṅkuśayutāruṇā

vetālaśākinīgṛdhrakākolūkaśivākulā

ādyāṣṭakamidaṃ khyātaṃ śeṣabhedastu vistaraḥ

danturākṣemakarī syādbhūmau jānukarāsthitā

eṅgā (?) hāsyā mahodaryo rūpiṇyo'psarasaḥ [atraiva kvaciccaṇḍaḥ ityāpi pāṭhaḥ citrapustake] sadā

yogeśvaroratha vīraśca ardhacandrāsane sthitaḥ

badhdvā vinyasya hṛdaye'thavottānakaradvayam

pracaṇḍo daṇḍadhārī ca kāryaścaṇḍeśvaro mahān

mahodaro vṛhaddeha ekadantastrilocanaḥ

divyāmbaradharaḥ kāryo nāgayajñopavītavān

ṣoḍaśāṅgulakaṃ dairghyācchuṇḍaṃ ṣaḍtriṃśadaṅgulam

pīnaḥ samunnatoraskaḥ kartavyo gaṇanāyakaḥ

kaṇṭhaguhayāntaraṃ kāryaṃ samaṃ dvātriṃśadaṅgulam

mastakaṃ niviḍaṃ vyastaṃ vāma [mū, a: vāta] kumbhasamunnatam

randhraṃ randhrāṅgulau kumbhau tanmadhye sūtramānataḥ

ekaikaṃ hrāsayogena yāvadvedāṅgulasthitiḥ

p. 115) pañcatālapradairghyaṃ ca [k, kh: pradairghyāṅga] catustālaviveśanam

svadantaṃ [g: sudaṇḍaṃ] dakṣiṇe pāṇau vāmahaste ca laḍḍukam

gaṇeśalakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ caturhastaṃ tu nānyathā [k, kh: stvato'nyathā; gh: tathānyathā]

bhujābhyāmadhikaṃ kuryātṣaḍbhujaṃ pūrvavacca tam [g: dvṛtam]

vāmaṃ cābhayadaṃ hastaṃ śubhaṃ cāṣṭabhujānvitam

padmādhastāttu kartavyo mahākhu [mū, a, g, gh: mahokṣa] ścārurūpavān

ṣaḍbhujaścāṣṭabāhuśca prahṛṣṭastāṇḍavākulaḥ

ādyaṃ sitaṃ gṛhe'bhīṣṭaṃ trividhaṃ suramandire

bhujairdvādaśabhiryuktaḥ skando mukhyaḥ surūpakaḥ

śāntyā ca dakṣiṇe haste ṣaḍbhirvāmakare tathā

gauṇaḥ kṣetre'thavā ṣaḍdorekavaktraḥ praśasyate

yathākramaṃ sadā ṣaḍbhirdakṣiṇottarapāṇibhiḥ

bibhrachaktiṃ kare yāmye tāmracūḍaṃ tathetare

śikhyārūḍhastu śārvo'yaṃ sarvālaṅkārabhaṣitaḥ

śilāhemādilohairvā vṛṣaṃ kuryādyathepsitam

sādhāraṇeṣu liṅgeṣu prastāvādvihito vṛṣaḥ

liṅgatryaṃśojhitaḥ kāryastaddvirāyativigrahaḥ

śubhonmolitadṛglakṣma liṅgamastakamastakaḥ

dairghyāccaturaśotyaśāscāṅgulaṃ tena kalpanam

aṣṭanetrapṛthutvaṃ syāproccaistadvaktradīrghatā

tantribhāgaṃ tu tārā syājjyotistatpañcamāṃśataḥ

nāsāṅgulapramāvṛttā [mū: pramodvṛttā; k, kh: bhramādvṛttā] dvinetraṃ śuṣirāntaram [mū: svaśirāntaram]

pañcanetraṃ tu vistāraṃ tadūrdhva tu vivardhate [mū: vivarjayet]

caturnetrobhayoḥ [mū, a, k, g: netre tayoḥ] pārśve vaktramānaṃ tu sandhitaḥ

tanmūle saptavistāraṃ karṇamūlasamanvitam

aṅgulārdhasamaṃ yāvanmūlaṃ pañcakavartulam [mū: vartanam]

karṇamūlāttu nāsārdhaṃ sūtrahāsānnayetsamam

śaṅkhamūla dvinetraṃ syādyathāgre netrapādakam

pārśvapiṇḍī dvinetrārdhaṃ ṣaḍnetrāgrāvaṭuḥ [g: śrāvaduḥ] smṛtaḥ

dvinetrocchrayatordhvaṃ [mū: ūrdhaṃ] syādvṛttamūrdhvaṃ [k, g: lajūṃ] yathā bhavet

sandhirnetrādhikaḥ [mū: sadvinetrārdhika] proktaścaturnetropajaṅghikā

gulphārdhaṃ khurikānetraṃ pādasyātparimāṇataḥ

caturdaśādi tadvaca trihīne copajaṅghrikā

ṣaḍnetrāntastathājñeyā tasmātsyātpucchasantatiḥ

tālatrayaṃ pradīrghaṃ syāttālārdhaṃ keśasaṃhatiḥ

tribhirvinā tathāgre syāddvinetraṃ kambalastvadhaḥ

triguṇāstu smṛto'nyatra nābhirnetradvayaṃ bhavet

jaṅghā netraṃ smṛta, sandhiḥ ṣaḍnetrā copajaṅghikā

khurike'ṅgulimānetu pañcanetratrayaṃ vinā

pṛṣṭhagatā ca vijñeyā dyaṅgulādhāṅgulaṃ yathā

trimukhā trikalāhāsaḥ pṛṣṭhataścopajaṅghikā

viśvakarmātpunaścānyaṃ vṛṣaṃ vakṣyāmi lāghavāt

kṣetraṃ vedāsrakaṃ kṛtvā navabhāgānprakalpayet

tribhāgairagrato [mū: rurdhvato] jaṅghā bhāgaikaṃ tūrdhvato haret

p. 120) hṛtvā [mū: kṛtvā] bhāgadvayaṃ cordhvaṃ kāryaṃ nāsālalāṭakam

tadadhoṃśāttathā luptvā karśoṃ kṛtvā tu śṛṅgavat

karṇādhaḥ koṇasūtraṃ tu dantordhvārdhahareddhruvam

sārdhabhāgadvayenaiva pṛṣṭhasya parikalpanam [k: parikalpanā]

bhāgoṃ'śaṃ gṛhnya cādhoṃśe tyaktvārdha koṇāsūtrataḥ

sphicaṃ pucchaṃ sakeśaṃ ca vartayenmatimānnaraḥ

jānurandhraṃ tathā sakthisandhiṃ jaṅgā khuraṃ budhaḥ

jñātvā lakṣmaṃ prakurvīta vṛṣaṃvṛṣa bharupakam

sākṣamālī triśūlī ca nandīśo dvārapālakaḥ

kūrcavān piṅgakeśaśca muṇḍamālī trilocanaḥ

kṛṣṇagātro mahākālo dvāre vai vāmataḥ sthitaḥ

vṛddharūpaḥ śikhī hṛṣṭaḥ kṣāmakukṣiḥ supāṇḍuraḥ

bhṛṅgirīṭīgaṇāścaiva nṛtyannityaṃ śivāgrataḥ

daṇḍī karaṇḍakopetaḥ kūṣmāṇḍaḥpūrvamānataḥ

kāryāste gajagokarṇā nānāvaktrā mahodarāḥ

lelihānaḥ susaṃkruddho jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍitaḥ

aṣṭādaśa bhujo bhīmo jvaladāyudhapāṇikaḥ

ninghandakṣiṇāhastaiśca pāparogamadhaḥ sthitam

ghaṇṭākuṭhārakaṃ vāmaiḥ vibhṛddvābhyāṃ triśūlakam

ghaṇṭāmālākulo devaḥ puṇḍarīkājināmbaraḥ

turagaiḥ saptabhiryukta ūrdhvaṃ tatrāsthito raviḥ

kapilo hemabhadraśca bhavaseno hayāḥ kvacit

anuṣṭuppaṅktirityuktāśchandāṃsi harayo raveḥ

sahasrāṃśurmahātejā maṇikuṇḍalamaṇḍitaḥ

sanālapatre rājīve bibhratskandhe kare kramāt

masībhājanalekhanyau dhārayanhastayordvayoḥ

bālavyajanadhāriṇyau rājñīchāye [saṃjñāchāye, iti sādhu pāṭho bhavet |] tu pārśvayoḥ [mū, a, g: pārścataḥ]

ākṛṣṭakaṇabāṇāca pārśce dakṣiṇake sthitā

athavāśvasamārūḍhaḥ kārya ekastu bhāskaraḥ

madhye kuṇḍayakṣamālāvān pūrvārdhe[k, kh, gh: mūrdhnyardha]vedikākaraḥ

anye yamādivanyahyantā[k, kh, g, gh: rakṣāntā]ścaturhastā dviṣaḍdale

mudgarasṛṇicakrasrugbhuṃto[mū: srugvanto]'gnyādividiksthitāḥ

varuṇaḥ sūryaṃnāmā ca sahasrāṃśustathāparaḥ

eter'kā dvādaśa jñeyā meṣādi [gh: jhaṣāni]rāśisaṃsthitāḥ

kapilaḥ pītavarṇaśca śukābho dhavalastathā

iḍā suṣumnā viśvārcirbindu saṃjñā pramardinī

nīlāmbarā dhanāntaḥ sthā amṛtāhvā ca śaktayaḥ

svadevarupavarṇāṃstāḥ kesarāgreṣu vinyaset

dakṣiṇo śūladhārī ca vāmahaste kapālabhṛt

dhārayannitare haste dvābhyāmabjau karānakaiḥ

caturbhiraṣṭabhirvāpi madhye'bje'ṣṭada[so'ṣṭa]lānvitam

p. 125) vedāṃśapātanātkoṇa marṭāśairatha kambalam [kh: kandharam]

tryaṃśapraveśanātkaṇṭhādekaikanirgamaṃ khuram

ghaṇṭāṃ ca mekhalāṃ caiva kuryāchṛṅgāṇi koṇataḥ

prālambyāsakta [mū: pālambyāyya] revanta [gh: revantu]śchatradhārādibhiryutaḥ

nānāratnasuveśāḍhyā [gh: sukhesyāḍhyā]ścaṇḍatejāḥ svabhāvajāḥ

p. 126) somaṃ saumyaṃ tathā śvetaṃ kuṇḍikājāpyamālinam

budhaṃ śyāmaṃ yuvānaṃ ca rūpiṇaṃ cāpapāṇinam

yāmyekākṣaṃ sitaṃ śukraṃ kuṇḍikāmantramālikam

vyāvṛttāsyaṃ sahāsyaṃ ca piṅgabhrūśmaśrulocanam

triphaṇaṃ [mū, a, k, kh: triphalaṃ] dhūmravarṇābhaṃ naranāgārdharūpiṇam [gh: naraṃ]

etāṃstu dvibhujānsarvānbhūṣaṇāmbarasaṃyutān

śakaṭaṃ medhameteṣāmadho yānaṃ kramātsadā

sitaḥ sahasrabhogo hīśvaraḥ [mū, a: hī śiraḥ] kuvalayānvitaḥ

kṛṣṇaḥ karkoṭakaḥ kaṇṭhe śulkarekhātrayānvitaḥ

śaṅkhavarṇo mahāpadmo mastake kṛṣṇaśūladhṛk [k: śūlakam]

kuliko raktadehastu candrārdha [g: candrārka] kṛtamastakaḥ

akṣasūtradharāḥ sarve kuṇḍikāpucchasaṃyutāḥ

bhṛtyāścaikaphaṇāḥ kāryāstadrūpā maṇivarjitāḥ

hārakuṇḍalakeyūraiḥ sadābhara[k: sarvābharaṇa]ṇabhūṣitā

īdṛśī manasā kāryā śrīkaṇṭhamanasambhavā

yogapaṭṭāvṛto'bjasthaḥ kuṇḍikā cākṣasūtrabhṛt [mū, a, k: dhṛk]

nānālaṅkārasaṃyuktāvutpalamaṇi bhṛtkarau

sudṛṣṭā [g: sukṛṣṇā] jalpamānā ca svotsaṅge putradhāriṇī

padmā [gh: parṇā] nandanapadmastha svāṅgādevī [gh: suṅgādevī] prakīrtitā

basukokakapidvikṣa [gh: dvikka] vyāghravyāghrāsyakaṃ kramāt

rakṣodvitayakumbhīra [kumbhīraḥ nakrābhidhojalavaraḥ | kintvasya citrodāharaṇe śukābhidhaḥ; pakṣiveśeṣo vartate | ślokānusāreṇa nakrābhidho jalacara viśeṣa eva bhavitumarhati |] sarpacakrāsyakaṃ [k, kh: nakrāsyakaṃ] ca bham

aśvinau ca yamo vahniḥ prajendrenduharo'ditiḥ

p. 129) tvaṣṭā vāyuḥ surendro'gnirmitrendrau yāvatpāmpatiḥ [mū: yātudhāmpatiḥ]

viṣṇurvasurajaikādipādahirbudhna [gh: rajaikādyahirbudhna] pūṣakāḥ

pumān gadī savīṇā ca yoṣitta [k, kh, gh: yoṣicca] nmithunaṃ dhanuḥ

strī naustholkāsibhṛtkanyā tulādhāro narastulā

meṣādyāḥ pañca caivānye [gh: evādye] svanāmatulyarūpakāḥ

hemābhaḥ sakirīṭaśca devarājaḥ surāvṛtaḥ

chāgasthaḥ [asya sthāne mṛgo vartate citrapustake] sākṣasūtrograḥ saptārciḥ śaktidhārakaḥ

raktahṛkpāśabhṛtkṛṣṇo mahiṣasthogradūtavān

puṃsthita khaḍgahastaśca nīlābho rākṣasāvṛtaḥ

sarvābharaṇasaṃyukto varūṇaḥ pītavastrabhṛt

raktakṣo [gh: raktāsyo] raktavāsaśca mṛgasthaścaṅkuśī dhvajī

dhaneśo varadaḥ kāryo nānābharaṇamaṇḍitaḥ

īśāno'hīndubhṛccaite lokapālā dvibāhavaḥ

viśvakarmā sadā gauro jñānamudrākṣasūtrabhṛt

ālīḍhakaraṇāsaktaṃ mahākāyaṃ subhīṣaṇam

pītadhvajadharaṃ vāme tarjanīṃ vā hṛdagrataḥ

saṃpīḍya rākasān padbhyāṃ muñcannādaṃ[mū, a, kh, g: padbhyāmuccapādaṃ]marutsutam

skandhayakṣānvitā nityaṃ kartavyā yoṣidanvitāḥ

mālābhṛto'thavā hṛṣṭāḥ khasthā vidyādharāḥ sadā

piṅgavarvara[mū: carcara]keśāśva vetālā vikṛtānanāḥ [g: vibṛtānanāḥ]

p. 132) kapālamālinaḥ śūladhārīṇaḥ kṣetrapālakāḥ

kṛśā mahodarāḥ pretā khalva[g: khalu]gallāśca lomaśāḥ

svāṅgavarṇadharāḥ sarvā yathoktaṃ brahmayāmale

piṅgākṣī cākṣayākṣapā [gh: kṣeya] ilā līlā layā tathā

hutāśā ca viśālākṣī huṅkārā vaḍavāmukhī

sarbajñā taralā tārā ṛgvedā tu hayānanā

raktākṣī suprasiddhā tu vidmujjihvā karaṅkiṇī

p. 133) candrā candravalī caiva prapañcā pralayāntikā

yamanī tapanī caiva vāmanī vikṛtānanā

yamajihvā jayantī ca durjayā ca yamāntikā

catuḥ ṣaṣṭiriyaṃ devyā mūrtibhedā nigadyate

hemābhā bhūṣaṇāḍhyā syādakṣobhyā karisaṃsthitā

dhanuḥ kapābhṛtsaumye ṝkṣasthā tarjanīyutā

pretasthā rāghasī gaurī kārdhakatarikānvitā

kapālaṃ ca phalaṃ saumye dhatte kubjanṛsaṃsthitā

vāme kapālapicchī [k, kh: piṇḍī] bhṛtsarvālaṅkāramaṇḍitā

kauśeyapāśabhṛdyāmye vāme cāṅkuśakheṭinī

kuṭhārāśanibhṛdyāmye saumye pāśāṅkuśānvitā

yāmye ḍamaruśūleṣumastahastākṣapā matā

raktā bahirsthitā tvīlā krīḍantī dahanaiḥ saha

bibhrāṇā paṭṭiśaṃ pāśaṃ vāme mastārdhamambujam

kartarīkārdhabhṛdvāme tarjanyākṛṣṭasṛkkikā

śūlaṃ ruruyutaṃ dvābhyāṃ dhatte lolā sūsārase (?)

laṅkeśorusthitā laṅkā carvantīṃ [mū: varṣantī] piśitaṃ ghanam

śoṇā laṅkeśvarī kuṇḍe ūrdhvabālāṃ ca [mū: ūrvālācaraṇe] bibhratī

bibhrāṇā kolagā raktā lālāsṛglālāsā matā

kartarīkumbhabhṛdyāme saumye pāśakapālinī [kh: kadhāriṇī]

vāme cābhayahastā syādviṣṭarājyaghaṭānvitā

kartaryabhayabhṛdyāmye vāme kaṇṭākapālinī

musalaṃ bibhratī yāmye somye ca phalapallavau

samatsya [gh: samatse]kārdhabhṛdvāme matsyamanyatra vāsitā

hāhāravā sitā krūrā rāsabhāsyā kharāsanā [mū, a, g, gh: kharānanā]

vāme masīghaṭīṃ pāśaṃ lekhanīdaṇḍabhṛttataḥ

vāme samadiraṃ pātraṃ [g: śūlaṃ] bibhratī jambuke sthitā

yāmye'syāḥ khiṅkhirīṃ (?) śūlaṃ kharparaṃ lelihānyataḥ

bibhrāṇobhājina [ibha arjinam iti cchedaḥ |] dvābhyāṃ sarvajñā pretagāsitā

śalaḍamaruhastā ca godhāgā taralāsitā

śavārdhaṃ bibhratī saumye śūlaṃ mugdaramanyataḥ

dakṣiṇo jāpyayāmālāṃ ca vāme pusta kamaṇḍalum

śūrpamuṇḍakarā yāmye'nyatra śephālikākabhṛt

khadvāṅgaṃ rurukaṃ saumye śūlolke bibhratī tataḥ

kaśūlāndhakakaṅkālabhṛdadvayo ścamavāsinī

vāme karoghadhāpāśaṃ[k, kh, g: dyathānāsaṃ; gh: pavānāsaṃ]ghaṇṭeśūlkadharānyathaḥ

p. 137) śaṅkhakheṭakahastogrā yāmye svastikakhaḍgabhṛt

gadādhvajadharā yāmye vāme pāśakapālabhṛt

cakrasthā cakrakaṭṭārīdhārīṇī [kh: cakravākaśca cakrasthā] dakṣiṇe kare

kumbhapāśadharāśvetā kroḍaputrā karaṅkiṇī

jalairvṛtā ghanārūḍhā taḍinmaṇḍalamaṇḍitā

kapālaṃ muṇḍamanyatra śatrughnāḥ sphaṭikatviṣaḥ

kālakarṇī jagatkhyātā karṇe kālavibhūṣaṇā

kartaryabhayabhradyāmye śūla [gh: pūra] pāśadharānyataḥ

kamaṇḍalukuśavyagrā cātrasūtrasruvānvitā

candrāvalī ca hemābhā kṛṣṇasāramṛgāsanā

gajayānā prapañcākhyā gaurī viśvaprapañcikā

p. 138) khādantyāmraphalaṃ nityaṃ kapyāsyā gaura varṇinī

bhindipālakakheṭābhyāṃ prāsāsibhyāṃ ca saṃyutā [gh: saṃyuge]

kaṃ phalaṃ kartarīṃ khaḍgaṃ bibhratī saumyayāmyayoḥ

kharasthā babhruvarṇa syātpiśitāśātidurbalā

dhvana iḍamaru[gh: dhvajaṃ ḍamaru]kartaryau pāśaṃ picchaṃ ca bibhratī

vamanī [g: ramaṇī] puṣpakasthā syādgaurī yakṣagaṇānvitā

svaphaṇā[k, g: suphaṇā]yāna bhāje [k: nubhogā; kh: dhārabhāge] ca nyastahastā bhavediyam

kuṭhāraṃ laḍḍukaṃ vāme'kṣamālī panasaṃ tataḥ

jihvābhṛdvajriṇī pītā caturdorvikṛtānanā

dvihaṃsavadrathasthā syāttālābhā vāyuvegikā

mahākāyā ca gauryaṅka[mū: gauryarka]kartarī bhṛnnṛkādanī

tūraṃ ḍamaruṃ yāmye'syāḥ śeṣe khaṭvāṅgamastakam

vīndrasthā [vīndrapakṣirājaḥ] viśvarūpā syāddhemābhā vanamālinī

bibhratī yamajihvākhyā karālā mahiṣasdhitā

sadaityamuṇḍaśūlogrā [li. pu: sadaityaśūlamuṇḍogrā] jayantī vāmahastayoḥ

nānābhūtāvṛtā śvetā durgakānanasaṃsthitā

śakaṭasthātidhorāsyā kṣīravarṇā yamāntikā [mū, a: yavāntikā]

vāme karaṃ [k, kh, g, gh: tūraṃ] ca khaṭvāṅgaṃ śūlaṃ ṭaṅkaṃ [gh: ghaṇṭaṃ] ca bibhratī

triśūlayaṣṭi [k, kh, g, gh, ṅ: kaṃ śalayaṣṭi]bhṛdyāmye bhindipālagadāsibhṛt

kartarīśūlabhṛtsūrye (saumye) vāme muṇḍakapālabhṛt

śūlaṃ ḍamaruṃ yāmye'syāḥ kārdhakhaṭvāṅgadhṛk tataḥ

kartavyāḥ śilpibhirnityaṃ yoginyo'śeṣasiddhidāḥ

khaḍgāṅkuśakuṭhāreṣu viśvābhayakṛdarkataḥ [gh: dakṣataḥ]

gajacarmadharo dvābhyāṃ kṛttivāso'hibhūṣitaḥ

pralayāmbudavarṇābho muṇḍakāpālamālinaḥ (kaḥ)

caṇḍākhyo mātṛmadhye tu saṃsthitaḥ pītakūrcabhṛt [gh: pīnakūrkhadhṛk]

tatṣaḍaṅgāni [g: taṃtsuta] jātyanataiḥ khānvitaṃ ca kramādyajet

nādabindvindusaṃyuktaṃ [k, g: bindusaṃyuktaṃ] mātṛnāthānta [kh: nāthāṅga]dīpitam

guhyadevānyarūpāṇāṃ sve sve śāstre viniścayaḥ

pañcadhā pratimāḥ khyātā mānataḥ saptadhā tathā

tatheśvarātsurā sarve kāryakāraṇabhedataḥ

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye rudrādi vyaktaliṅga vartanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭo vidhiḥ

pañcāsyādi yathāliṅgaṃ bhavetsuvyaktapañcakam [mū, a: lakṣaṇam]

pañcavaktramaniṣṭeṣu caturvaktraṃ sukhāvaham

ekavaktraṃ syādrogaghnaṃ nādhikāmyaṃ calācalam

caturmukhaṃ tu khādhāre [gh: mukhe sukhādhāre; k: tu prasāde] pañcāsyaṃ vā caturmukham

liṅgamūrdhnisthita vaktramaiśānaṃ tvīśamīkṣakam [mū: vīkṣakam]

siṃhāsyaṃ [gh: siṃhāghraṃ] śmaśrukeśāḍhyaṃ maindraṃ tatpurūṣānanam

daṃṣṭrākarālaṃ vṛttākṣaṃ jvalatkeśādibhūṣitam

kāntālaṅkārakeśādi [k, kh: keśāṃḍhya; g: keśāśca] tilakolakamaṇḍitam

tryakṣaṃ pīnaṃ yuvākāraṃ suprasannaṃ suśobhamam

sadyaḥ puṃbhyāṃ dvivaktraṃ syātpuruṣeṇaikavaktrakam

tatvairliṅgaṃ grahairbhakte hṛtvāmbhoṃśo vidikṣu ca

digvibhāgena tadvaktraṃ [mū: tadvaktaṃ] bhūtavedānanaṃ sadā

tadvaddyāsye dvimātrākhyamekāsye tritrikāṅgulān

hṛtvā koṇeṣu randhrāṃśairliṅgaṃ ṣaṭtālaveṣṭitam

([cāpacinhāntargato'yaṃ pāṭho g. pustake nāsti]dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ ca tatrāsyaṃ liṅgaṃ saptāsyaveṣṭitam

navatyaṅgulakaṃ [gh: tryaṅgulakaṃ] vṛttādekāsyaṃ pūrvavaktrakam

p. 143) tadadhaḥṣaḍvibhāgena śivavaktrāṇi vartayet

cibukoṣṭhau vibhāgena [mū, a, k, kh: ca bhāgena] nāsālalāṭastakam

liṅgapārśce dvibhāgena bhāgaṃ tyaktvā tadūrdhvataḥ

pañtrāsyādyekavaktrāntamayameva vidhiḥ punaḥ

pañcānanādikaṃ proktaṃ suvyaktamadhudanocyate

dvibāhūnyūrdhvadehāni viṣṇavantacaraṇāni ca

pūrvakāye pumāṃsaṃ tu mātuluṅgākṣaśalinam [k, kh: sūtritam]

stryākāramuktare guhyaṃ darpaṇotpalasaṃyutam

suvyakta pañcadhāhyevamāsyovatābidhisaṃsthitam [mū: saṃyutam]

evaṃ niṣpādya liṅgāni piṇḍikāṃ vartayettataḥ

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye vyaktāvyaktaliṅgānāṃ daśadhā vartanaṃ nāma saptamo vidhiḥ athoṣṭama

mahatyāḥ prātamāyāśca digliṅgādeśca sādhane

navajñāṃ kārayettasmātkadācidubhayorapi

piṇḍikā tu samākhyātā na hīnā nādhikā śubhā

mekhalā tattribhāgena khātaṃ tattryṃśahīnakam

madhye vṛttaṃ samālikhya vartayetkhātamādarāt

arvāg dairghyatribhāgena mekhalāvartanaṃ bahiḥ

mukhe tadardhavistāraṃ tantribhāge'mbunirgamaḥ

kaṇṭhādūrdhvaṃ sadā hyevamanyathā doṣadāyakam

ṣaṣṭhāṃśaveśanātkaṇṭhaścalānāṃ pañcamena vā

avyakte miśrake svāṃśe [mū: bhyāṃśe; g: dyāṃśe] brahmaviṣṇu[kh, gh: viṣṇo]svarūpataḥ

liṅgasthaulyapramāṇena samāsraṃ digvidiksthitam

samantājjyeṣṭhaliṅgasya yavaiḥ ṣaḍbhirvivardhitam

liṅgavannirmalā śuddhā kartavyā piṇḍikā budhaiḥ

śeṣāṇāṃ liṅgānāmardhārdha hrāsayedyavam

kāmānāṃ vasutastasya bhedān brūmaḥ samāsataḥ

pīṭhikāyāḥ samucchrāyaṃ vikārāṃśena bhājayet

guṇābhāge tathā kumbhaḥ kumbhapaṭṭi tu bhāgataḥ

pakṣabhāge [k, kh: pakṣābhāge] jalādhāra ekāṃśe śeṣapaṭṭikā

bāṇamatānusāreṇa kanyasī [kanīyasītyarthaḥ]tthaṃ payodharā

paṭṭopapaṭṭikā kaṇṭho galapaṭṭaṭdayupapaṭṭikā

mekhalā paṭṭikā kintu bhāgakena tadūrdhvataḥ

ucchrāyārdha tu pīṭhyāḥ (ṭhī) syādrudrabhāge dvidhā kṛte

tribhiraljaṃ dvipaṭṭau (hi) tu dvābhyāṃ dvābhyā tathāmbujam

dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ kramā[mū, a, k: tathā]tpadyaṃ sārdhāśe galanirgamaḥ

kaṇṭhadvayena [mū: kathadvayena] saṃyuktā ṣaḍbhiḥ padmairvibhūṣitā

samvartākhyā jalādhārā viṣṇubhāgāntamucchritā

liṅganāhatriveṣṭena vṛttāvyaktasya piṇḍikā

taddīrghatvārdhavistārāttadardhānnālakocchrayau

ucchraye daśadhābhakte padaṃ kumbhaṃ tathā galam

khuraṃ jaṅyā ca kūrṇauṃ ca kaṇṭhaṃ caivopakaṇṭhikām [k, kh: pakarṇikām]

ekayugmaikapakṣaikayugmaikagalāṃśakaiḥ

ṣoḍaśāṃśakṛtocchāye jyeṣṭhānāmāsane sadā

caraṇapaṭṭikākumbha paṭṭikā galapaṭṭikā

aṣṭadigravihastaiśca hīnamadhyottamāstribhiḥ

praveśogdamabāhulyācchū bhranālādi [kh: mānādi] pūrvavat

tattryaṃśātpīṭhavistārastatpādonasamucchrayaḥ

saha kumbho galo vṛtto vedāsrā paṭṭikā kramāt

([cāpāntargato'yaṃ pāṭhaḥ kh. pustake nāsti] anyatra tvevamevaṃ syātsaṃvṛttātkarṇa[gh: saṃvṛttāhvaṃtu]pīṭakam

suślakṣṇāśliṣṭasandhiśca rupeṇāmbudharādivat

keṣāṃcittu mate vyaktaliṅge tu chatramastake

tasmālliṅgānugaṃ pīṭhaṃ kartavyaṃ kartṛjīvane

yadi syāt pīḍyate rogaiḥ kartā rāṣṭraṃ gurūstathā [gh: stadā]

ucchrayeṇa triliṅgānāṃ na nyūno nādhiko hareḥ

munisārdhādhikaiḥ śambhuṃ kuryādāḍhyaṃ [k: dādyaṃ; gh: dārḍhyaṃ] vasorharim

pādonāṣṭāṃśato brahmā kāryaṃ cānāḍhyakaṃ kvacit

ekaṃ surārcite liṅge brahmā saptāṃśake dhruvam

sitādivarṇāvarṇānāṃ [k, g: pītādivarṇa] pīṭhādyā na vivarṇajāḥ

ratnajā vātha lohairvā viśeṣātkīrtaye'dhunā

vaiḍūryādyuttame nityaṃ hemajā piṇḍikā varā

bhāgadrayamathikaṃ vā vinyaset piṇḍikāvaṭau

mekhalādivibhāgo'syāḥ pūrvavatsamudāhṛtaḥ

na nyūnaṃ nādhikaṃ mānātśobhanaṃ [mū, a, kh: sbatalaṃ; gh: sutalaṃ] śvabhramiṣyate

pīṭhadoṣātkṣayo nāryāḥ kāro kartuṃśca sarvadā

bhuktimuktyubhayaprāptyai anyaḥ [mū, a, g: prāptau anyaḥ] kāmyasya hetave

padmārdhendugaṇākhyā ca kāmadā śubhamastake

dvimekhalā ca vedāsrā bhadrā yogarddhidāyinī

ṣaḍasrāyuḥ pradā vajrī mekhalātritayānvitā

maṇḍalā pūrṇacandrābhā tvarthadā pañcamekhalā

candrāmburuparāmai [k: ke dharā vāme] kāgnidyaṃśaistithi bhājitaiḥ

vṛttā padmapuṭākārā karṇiko bhayacumbitā

aṣṭakoṇā gaṇākhyātā [mū: khyā tu] muktidā [gh: bhuktidā] syāddvimekhalā

pūrvoktamekhalāgartanālakādisamanvitā

vistarokchrayanālādi sarbaṃ pūrvoṃditaṃ kramāt

saṃveṣṭya vartayetpiṇḍīṃ kartuḥ kāmaprasādhikam

mārūtīṃ dhānadīṃ raudrīṃ vaiṣṇāvīṃ brahmajāṃ sadā

sūtramadhye'rdhasūtreṇa matsyān koṇeṣu sarvataḥ

vedāsraṃ piṇḍikākṣetraṃ kuṇḍānāmapi sarvadā

madhyānmadhyagataṃ sūtraṃ [k, kh: sūtraiḥ] koṇaṃ saṃveṣdya cāparam

p. 151) tatsūtradvayasaṃyogātsādhayedvā triparṇikām [mū, a: bodhiparṇikām]

saṃbhrāmya madhyataḥ sūtraṃ sādhayedardhacandrikām

rakṣo'gnisaumyakāṣṭhāsu ta trisūtrya[k, kh: trisatryā]trikoṇikām

madhyādhasūtrasaṃbhrāntaiḥ piṇḍikā vartulā [gh: vartanād] bhavet

turyāsravṛttayormadhye yamandvoḥ sūtrapātanam

bhavetṣaḍasrikāpiṇḍī śeṣarekhāvithopanāt [kh, gh: lekhāvilopanāt]

sūtramadhyārdha sabṛttādabjā syāt [g: dabjābhā] pūrvacoditā

madhyasūtrārdha bhāgena [kh, gh: dvibhāgena] koṇāni hrāsayedatha

koṇaiḥ [gh: koṇā] sūtraparicchinnaiḥ kuryādaṣṭāsrikāṃ sadā

saṃchidyāsrīṃśca saṃchidya kṛtvā tu daśamāstrikam [k, gh: daśabhāṃśikīm]

kartavyā ṣoḍakāṃśaistu [gh: śaivaṃ] piṇḍikā keśavāśrayā [gh: ścaye]

pūrvavatyoḍaśaṃśaistu kuryād dvātriṃśadasrikām

āgneyī bodhipatrābhā pintarogāgnidāhakṛt

trikoṇā rākṣasājāṃ tu deśo[k: dveṣo; gh: doṣo] cchadakarī sadā [gh: tusā]

vāyavyā ca ṣaḍasroktā śatrūccāṭanakārikā

raudrī tvaṣṭāsrikā [g: raṣṭāśrikā] jñeyā muktidā sārināśinī

dvātriṃśadasrikā vāhnī svargākhyāphaladāyinī

ardhacandropamā nīlaṃ śeṣaḥ kodaṇḍavadbhavet

piṇḍīmāmastakādīnāṃ karotyevaṃ vidhaṃ phalam

bhagaliṅgāṅkita sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram

śivaliṅgaṃ paraṃ taccaṃ tayoryogā[gh: tayoryāgāt]nmahatphalam

dalāṅghrigartikā kūrmoṃ [mū, gh: kūrma] vedāsrā valiṅgartinī

caturasraṃ ta[gh: rasrayaṃśataṃ]dgartaṃ syād brahmāṃśa[gh: brahyāṅgaṃ]tu prakalpitam

tadpādaśvabhrikā sāvjā grahagartā śilā bhavet

kāryā brahmaśilānyārthi[gh: nyo'rtho]bṛttā tryasrā tu[gh: tryasrānta] sādhane

ekajaṃ caika varṇaṃ vā śubhametadato'śubham

p. 154) mūlyaṃ samavadhāryādau vaṇijo daiśikottamaḥ

paścādijādvipāṣaṇḍāṃ stato mūlyaṃ prakāśayet

tadārdhenādhvaraṃ kṛtvā liṅgaṃ sthāpyaṃ suvāsare [mū, a, g: surāsure]

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye pīṭhavartanaṃ nāmāṣṭamo vidhiḥ

vedāsre grahabhakte [k, kh, gh: vedāsraṃ grahabhaktaṃ] syānmadhye śūlaṃ tu randhrataḥ

tyaktvā [gh: tyājyā] sārdhau dvivistārau ṣaḍbhirdirdhau bhramānmatau

tadghṛttau dvyardhadīrdhau ca kartavyau ca suśobhanau

vedyadhastāttridhā dairghyāddaṇḍaḥ kāryastrivistara

site rekhā bhaveddaṇḍa aiśyāmeva triśūlakam

madhye śṛṅgaṃ tu pītaṃ vā harite pakṣaśaṅgake

evaṃ saṃkṣepato vajraṃ khyātamindrasya pūrvataḥ

lohitaṃ madhyamaṃ śṛṅgaṃ [k, kh: śūlaṃ] vahneḥ śaktistadā diśi

mūle cāgre ca daṇḍasya kāryo vṛttāgramūlataḥ

vedādvahiḥsitā rekhā[mū, a: vedhā]kṛṣṇā yāmye yamasya tu

bṛttāgraṃ pādasaṃveśātkoṇamūlaṃ [kh, gh: kāmamūlaṃ] suśobhanam

valkalam khaṇḍacandrābhaṃ tanmadhyordhvadvisārdhata [mū, a, k, kh: madhye'rdha]

tatsame'pyunnataṃ madhye mukhaṃ tanmūlapakṣayoḥ

muṣṭiṃ saṃvartayedevaṃ pītaṃ vā sitarūpakam

muṣdyucchrayāccaturdhāto dīrghaḥ khaḍgāgrabartulaḥ

bhṛṅgābhaṃ tryaṃśasantyāgātkhaḍgo nairṛtarākṣase

prasaṅgādgaditaṃ śastramakṣobhya yoginīpriyam

mukhapucchārdhamādyante digdeśairyugamadhyataḥ

bhrāmya dvau dvau dvibhāgābhyāṃ madhyakoṣṭhadvaye budhaḥ

granthirūpaṃ yathā samyak tathā tatparilopayet

tadadhoṃśena [kh: 'ṅgena] vaktraṃ syānnāsākṣirasanānvitam

tadadhaḥ pārśvayordyaṃśe karṇasūtraṃ dvayoḥ samam

tryaṃśādho hrāsayetpucchaṃ svāṃśaṃ [gh: svāṃśa] tryaṃśena vāṇataḥ

sito'hiḥ kṛṣṇajihvāsyo raktākṣaśca suśobhanaḥ

vārūṇyāṃ vārūṇaḥ pāśaḥ kartavyo diśi daiśikaḥ

vāyukoṇe'mbutaḥ proktaṃ[k, kh: koṇāmbutatproktaṃ]tryaṃśena tu ca darpaṇam [mū: tadarpaṇam]

candrārdhātkoṇato dyaṃśaṃ lopayejjalata[mū: dyantataḥ]stataḥ

adho bhūtena vistārāttiryaksūtrāddvirūrdhvataḥ

taddyaṃśena ṝjuṃ kuryād dhvajaṃ vā kuṭilāhivat

nairṝtyātpañcaveśena sūtraṃ vinyasya koṇataḥ

īśānātsaptapūrveṇa saptaveśādbhramātpunaḥ

śeṣarekhā [mū, a, k, kh, g, ṅ: śeṣaveśā] vināśena vāyordhvajaśca mārute

śiro[ṅ: śito; k, kh: padmaṃ]darpaṇakhaṇḍendurdhvajaḥ pīto'thavā harit

ādyante sambikā dvābhyāṃ vṛttamekena pārśvayoḥ

pītā gadāruṇai rekhā madhyarekhā śukaprabhā

kaṭikāṅghrisamā vṛttā catasro rekhikāstathā

kuverāstraṃ bhavedevaṃ somakāṣṭhāsthitaṃ sadā

suśvetaṃ raktanemyāḍhyamīśendrāntarasaṃsthitam

nābhimadhyāntato'śena [gh: madhyāttu tāṃśena] mekhalā bhramato bhavet

tadvattatparatoṃ'śena bhramāccakraṃ tu vartulam

aṣṭadikṣvārakāstvaṣṭau kkacidvā ṣoḍaśārakāḥ

cakrādhaḥ prāntatattryaṃ[k, kh: madhyato dyaṃśā]śādvivistārādrasabhāgikam [kh: masūrī cāsatonnatā]

ekenoccā pravistārā paṭṭikā caturasrikā

cakrasyāsanamevaṃ tu raśmayaḥ paritastataḥ

p. 164) sacandratārakāyoge [mū: vyacandratārakāyoge; k: candratārakayoge tu] sulagnakaraṇānvitate

p. 9) sra'vastragandhadhūpādyairdaivajñaṃ saṃprapūjayet

ghaṭikāśaṅkuyogena pratiṣṭhālagnamādiśet

sarvopāyānvitaṃ puṇyaṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadam

yannasādhyaṃ tu vedeṃṣu tatsusādhyaṃ śivāgame

nacaitatkāraṇaṃ [gh: caitaiḥ-] vedaiḥ pratiṣṭhāyāḥ śivāgame

devyāmatātsamākṛṣya likhitaṃ leśato mayā

śodhayitvā tato liṅgaṃ rathe tvāropya yatnataḥ

ākarṣayecca nāgāśvairmahiṣairvā bṛṣai ratham

avatāryāgataṃ liṅgaṃ syandanasthaṃ prayatnataḥ

aṣṭāṅgasaṃskṛtaṃ cārghaṃ dattvā liṅgaṃ praveśayet

hemapātrādigairetairarghaṃ dadyā dathāṣṭabhiḥ

pādaśaucaṃ tata kṛtvā pāduke sthāpayetpunaḥ

kuṅkumāgurukarpūrairlimpeta samacandanaiḥ

mukuṭaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ caiva hārakeyūrakaṅkaṇau

gṛhasthānāṃ vidhau samyagvittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet

pradhānaśilpinaṃ tadvagdandhapuṣpaistathetarān

bhavatpādaprasādānme kṛtsnaṃ yāgaphalaṃ bhavet

pādalagno vadetkartā kuru [gh: kuyuḥ] (rve) karmatvadājñayā

prakṣālya pāṇipādaṃ ca triḥ pibedambu vītritam

saṃhitābhistribhiḥ pūrvamāsyamevamupaspṛśet

aṅgūṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu cakṣuḥśrotre punaḥ punaḥ

sarvābhistu śiraḥ paścādvāhū cāgreṇa saṃspṛśet

yāgālayaṃ praviśyātha khaḍgabījaiḥ surecitaiḥ

bhūtaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kuryānmūrtyādisakalīkṛtam

dvādaśāṅgulamutsiktaṃ sitoṣṇīṣavidhārakaḥ

bhavedyajñe sadācāryaḥ śrīkaṇṭharūpadhārakaḥ

ityevaṃ nirvikalpena cetasā cintayetsadā

tenārdhavāriṇā prokṣya sarvaṃ puṣpaphalādikam

varmaṇā saṅgahetpaścāccāmarairvātha picchakaiḥ [gh: piñjakaiḥ]

gandhāmbuphalaratnāḍhyamoṣadhīvastrasaṃyutam [gh: maṇḍitam]

navāṃśo'rdhaparityakte ṣaṣṭhe bhāge tridhā kṛte

yavākārebhanetrābhā pakṣasūtraṃ vinā bhavet

tyakttvādho dvyekabhāgaṃ tu dvau caivādho'ṣṭadhā punaḥ

adho bhāgadvayaṃ muktvā saṅgamaḥ pṛṣṭhatastayoḥ

agre muniparityakte lakṣaṇaṃ syātphaṇisthite

āgho vihāya [mū: vidhāya] bhāgau dvau melakaṃ syāttayoḥ sadā

pūjābhāge vikārāṃśe rekhādhaḥ saptavarjite

yasyottare [k, g: yasyāntare] 'bjikākārā saṅgati pṛṣṭhamadhyataḥ

pakṣasūtre paraṃ kāryaṃ tadadho lambanaṃ haret

tasmātsyādviṣṇusamparkaḥ pakṣasūtre'va [kh: sūtre ca] lambane

chatrābhaṃ kukkuṭāṇḍe tu bālendvābhe yavopamam

yaśo rājyaṃ dhanaṃ putraṃ saubhāgyaṃ jīvitaṃ kramāt

praṇālasyārdhavistārātpīṭhāvaṭakalendu vā

digīśānāṃtu liṅgānāṃ svakāmaphalasādhane

śūlaṃ vajraṃ ca śaktiṃ ca tikṣṇāgraṃ kambukaṃ tathā

snigdhā samā ca gambhīrā ṛjvī sarvārthadā bhavet

sarvameva śirasthatvamīśānasya hi lakṣaṇam

haraḥ

urvitattvaṃ samākhyātaṃ jalādidviguṇaṃ [kh: digguṇaṃ] kramāt

sparśaḥśabdaśca pañcaiva tanmātrāṇi yathākramam

pumān rāgaśca vidyā ca niyatiśca kulāntakaḥ [mū: kalāntakaḥ]

śaktiścebhyaḥ parastācca śivākhyaḥ paramo'vyayaḥ

pañcabhūtaṃ ca tanmātraṃ buddhiḥ karmendriyaṇi ca

caturviṃśatitattvāni prakṛtirvāmakarṇagāḥ

samākhyātā niyatirhṛdaye sthitā

pañcaviṃśacca tattvāni piṇḍīkāyāṃ sthitāni tu

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye'dhivāsanaṃ nāma pañcadaśo vidhiḥ
