tataścordhvaikabhāgena grīvā dvibhāgavistarā

tadūrdhvo'rdhāṃśataḥ kālaḥpadmaṃ dvivistarocchrayam

sacūlyādūrdhvataḥ padmo ghaṭāsyaṃ cāmrapallavi

ratnanyāsādi homaśca mantranyāsādi pūrvavat

sthāpayenmantra [mū, a: mata] bhedataḥ

dvyaṃśaṃ tryaṃśaṃ samastaṃ ca jyeṣṭhādikramataḥ kvacit

prakṣipya kalaśāḥ kāryāḥ kvacidīśādi yatnataḥ

viṣṇudhāmni ca cakreṇa anyeṣāṃ svāyughairapi

vāmā jyeṣṭhāhvayā raudrī svaśaktitrayakalpitā

mantrataḥ

karīraṣaṣṭhabhāgena cakraṃ syātsama [kh: sa ca] vartulam

cūliketaradevānāṃ sthāpanīyāḥ pramāṇataḥ

indīvaradalābhā sā svabījaiścūlikā sadā

gṛhyate sakalā nityaṃ dānahomādibhiḥ saha

tathā dharmaphalārthāya dhāmavṛkṣadhvajena tu

nānāgamānusāreṇa leśatastaṃ vadāmyataḥ

dhāmno dviguṇadairghyaḥ syātsārdhārdhāttatsamo dhvajaḥ

sarvārthaśāntaye śuklaḥ kāmārthe dikpatiprabhaḥ

svadevarūpakaiścitraḥ svaśaktyā śobhitaḥ kvacit

śirbhkau]

śāntihome kṛte saukhyaṃ dakṣiṇāsyena [mū: dyena] nānyathā

aiśāne vā pradeśe ca mārute vānyathāpare

utta [mū: ukta] mādidhvajādhāraṃ mastakārdhena vā kvacit

bhuvaneśānpunastatra vighnanāśāya siddhaye

samyāgdhyātvā [kh: jñātvā] tu tānsarvāṃstatastvāropayedguruḥ

aghoramatha śaivaṃ vā yadvā pāśupataṃ mahat

gandhādi brahmamuṇḍena dadyādvidyādhipena vā

dhyāyetsūryasahasrābhaṃ pralayāmbudanisvanam

tryakṣaṃ taḍillātajihvaṃ dīptabhrūśśrumūrdhajam

dhvajasyārohaṇaṃ kuryādupacāraṃ ca daiśikaḥ

dhvajāgre tu gururlagna iṣṭamantraṃ japaṃstadā [mū: japettadā]

yajamānastato lagno bhāryāputrakulaiḥ saha

yathā vidhūyate vātairdhvajaḥ prāsādamastake

rāṣṭre śāntirjayī rājā sukhinyaḥ syuḥ prajāstathā

jīrṇoddhāre kṛte karturdhvajānte maulikaṃ phalam

viṃśovidhi

āśrayantīha satvāni bhūtavetālarākṣasāḥ

kurvanti doṣasaṅghyātaṃ yatra rāṣṭreṣu te [g: pure] sthitāḥ

durbhikṣaṃ dāruṇaṃ rogaṃ prajānāśaṃ daridratām

pīḍā gobrāhmaṇādīnāṃ rājño duḥkhaṃ kuśīlatām

śatruvyasanapīḍā ca cintā ca mahatī bhavet

tasmāduddharaṇaṃ kāryaṃ vidhinā mantrapūrvakam

munmādaye] ddhruvam

gobrāhmaṇaprajānāṃ ca svarāṣṭrasya ca vṛddhaye

sunakṣatre dine yoge sadvāre kartṛśobhane

pracchannaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ ramyaṃ jaladvāraika [kh: dhāraika] toraṇam

pūrvavadvedikāṃ ramyāṃ śivakumbhāstrarakṣitām

bālukāpūrite pīṭhe sthaṇḍile pūjayeddharam

śivaliṅgadharānpañca bhojayed ghṛtapāyasai

dakṣiṇāṃ vittato dadyād brūyuḥ śaṃ rāṣṭrabhūpayoḥ

kuṇḍe tu vartule

mūrtibhṛdbhyastato dānaṃ bhūtadigdevatābalim

labdhānujño [kh: natvānujño] mahādevātkuryālliṅgādikoddhṛtim

yadatra saṃsthitaṃ satvaṃ tyaktvā cānyatra yātu tat [k: yāntvataḥ]

tato'bhiṣicya liṅgādyamastrajaptena [mū: mantraṇajaptena] vāriṇā

utthāpane prabadhnīyādityuktaṃ śambhunā kvacit

vṛṣayoḥ kakude (di) baddhvā varmaṇā bālatantunā

īśāśādīḥ kramātsarvāḥ khādakena guruḥ khanet

pāśasīrayugaṃ [kh: sārayuta] kuryādbalavantau tathā vṛṣau

dhvajadūṣitam]

sthūlaṃ vā durniviṣṭaṃ ca yacca pīḍākaraṃ bhuvi

eṣāṃ kuryāccikitsāṃ ca vyāghitasyeva bheṣajam

kriyamāṇe na doṣo'sti mantravarjitayā [tatā] yataḥ

pakvamṛcchailaratnotthaṃ kṣipedvāmena nityake

lohaṃ sarvaṃ viniṣpāṭya punastatraiva vinyaset

kṣipet

aduṣṭaṃ vidhinoddhṛtya sthāpayedyāgapūrvakam

brahmaśilādikaṃ sarvaṃ sthāpayetpūrvavatkramāt

liṅgādi cāparaṃ sthāpyamadhike vā phalaṃ bahu

tāvatkalpasahasrāṇi tatkartā tatpure vaset

dviguṇaṃ cāśmajānāṃ ca sīsakānāṃ tato'dhikam

dviguṇaṃ ratnajānāṃ tu ratnādhikye'dhikaṃ phalam

ghoreṇa vā mahāstreṇa dviguṇaṃ dhāmacālane

tvaritaṃ mriyate jantū rauravaṃ ca mṛto vrajet

dārupaṭṭāvṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ kṛtvā tu ghaṭayedgṛham

siddhe dhāmnyaṅgasaṃyogaṃ kuryādākṛṣya khaḍgataḥ

acchidrapūrṇatā yena syādaghoraśatena tu

liṅgādi sthāpitaṃ [sthāpakaṃ] yacca tatkṣamasva mamopari

asmākaṃ śilpināṃ cāpi suprīto bhava he bhava [gh: tava]

taṃ khaḍgaṃ kaṅkaṇaṃ dhenuṃ dadyātsopaskarau vṛṣau

ekahastaṃ samārabhya aṣṭahastāvadhikramāt

lokapālāyudhopetaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sārvadaivikam

yaccatuṣkoṇagāḥ śaṅkhāḥ śrīvatsākhyaṃ herestu tat

athānyannavadurgādi navanābhaṃ taducyate

tathā

padmamānaṃ [mū: mālaṃ] bhaveddvāramekena prativāraṇā

putrāyurdhanavṛddhidam [kh: vardhanam]

gairikeṅgudasindūrai raktamiṣṭa [kh: siktha] kayāthavā

śitaiḥ

sitābhyāmasitāṃśena aikyaṃ syāddhūmra [gh: dhūma] varṇakam

na hīnā nādhikāvakrā kāryā rekhā suśobhanā

vakrāyāmarthanāśaḥ syād vyastasthe varṇake ripuḥ

aśeṣaguṇadaṃ tasmātsthaṇḷiṃ vālukāmayam

tayoḥ sthānāni puṇyāni kathayāmi mahītale

sandohe nimnagākūle siddhakṣetreṣu [gh: sthāneṣu] sarvataḥ

mālyavāñśṛṅginīlau ca ṛkṣaśceme'drināyakāḥ

śrīśailaścaiva vindhyaśca mahendraśca kulācalāḥ

virajaścairuḍaḥ [mū: rutaḥ] puṇḍra ścopakṣetrāṇyamūni ca

māyāpuryatha jālākhyamete sandohasaṃjñakāḥ

pañcanadyatha sindhuśca sarasvatyaṣṭanimnagā

antarvedī pratiṣṭhānaṃ yājñiko deśa iṣyate

karavīro mahākālaḥ saugandhyupavanaṃ [kh: paraṇaṃ] vanam

bāṇasvayaṃbhuliṅgānāṃ padmakhaṇḍataḍāgayoḥ

siddhadevālayo grāma nagaraṃ kheṭakaṃ puram

puṇyasthānamathānyacca siddhasthānānyamūni ca

gṛhṇīyādbhūmimādau tu śāsanena krayeṇa vā

ādau kurmādyateḥ sthānamīśādīnāṃ tato yataḥ

maṭhamāśritya ye liṅgamarcayanti kubuddhayaḥ

yadīcchennarakaṃ gantuṃ putrapautrādibhiḥ saha

mahī

ripurmānaṃ sukhaṃ ca syānmadhyamā sarvataḥ plavā

bhaṅgaḥ pīto'rtha ijyā [mū: iṣṭa] syātpūrvādervasudhāplavāt

sajñakaḥ]

nadīpraveśitā [kh: ṣṭitā] śastā pūrvādi paścime'pi vā

vaṭaḥ prācyāṃ yamāśāyāṃ yajñāṅgo bodhirabdiśi

jayantīdāḍimīśyāmāpunnāgāriṣṭapādapā

bahudarbhavatī viprā kṣatriyā ca śarākulā

phalapuṣpavatīsnigdhā snigdhagulmoṣadhīdrumāḥ

ekavarṇā ghanā tulyā yamāmbudiśi connatā

candanāgurukāśmīracandrābjotpalacampakāḥ

śālībhamadadadhyājyadugdhānyetatsugandhinī

grīṣmakāle ca yā śītā uṣmaṇā śiśire'nviyā

śubhātmanā]

miṣo vṛṣā]

sā mahī]

āṣāḍhā kṛttikā jyeṣṭhā tathā śatabhiṣā ca bham

svajāti brāhmaṇī vāpi yathālābhaṃ ca gṛhyate [mū: pūjyate]

raktāṅgāraśilāyuktā [k: randhrā-] kṛmikīṭavatī ca yā

seṣṭakācitikeśāsthibhasmavalmīkaśarkarā [k, kh: jarjarā]

bahvāpadbahuvarṇā ca vajrāgnihyaricauradā [mū, ṅ: ṣairadā]

yoṣidduścāriṇī tatra rujo rājabhayaṃ bhavet

kākakaṅgoṣṭraśabdā vā sarvaiṣāmasuravāvahā

saśilpikā [kh: samalpikā]

nyūnā samādhikā jñeyā hīnamadhyottamā kṣitiḥ

bhuvaḥ

dvijādīnāṃ sitādyāśca jvalitāvartayo niśi

nirvāpyaḥ [mū: nirvāntyaḥ] prajvalantyaśca svajātisukhaduḥkhadā

tīśaśambarai]

sapta pañca trirātre syāddhīnādi [k, kh: dviśādi] rohaṇātridhā

vaiśyāya ca ṣaḍaṃśāsrā śūdrāya hyāyatā śubhā

ṣaṭkoṇikā ca yā

mṛtyurvyādhistathā pāpaṃ kramāddadyātpalaṃ mahī

śakaṭā dvipa [gh: dvija] pṛṣṭhābhā diṅmūḍhā ca karoti vai

śokaṃ ca sarvanāśaṃ ca sarvalokakṣayaṃ kramāt

yavapipīlikāmadhyā dundubhīpaṭahodarī

nānādoṣakarā hyetāḥ sarveṣāmeva varjitāḥ

prāgvidhānena sā grāhyā yadi nopahatā bhavet

yā sevitā sadā pṛthvī koṣṇā [k: koṣo] sṛgvajradūṣitā

api grāhyā [k: grāhya] guṇāḍhyā sā gṛhadhāmnoḥ suśodhitā

pakvaśasyena saṃskṛtya ghaṭayeddhāma [kh: vāsa] veśmanī

tataḥ prasādhayetkāṣṭhāṃ gṛhādīnāṃ taducyate

mīnayogena kauberīṃ dhruvayogena cānayet

nyasettanmadhyataḥ śaṅkuṃ khādiraṃ dvādaśocchrayam

tacchāyāyāḥ praveśena paścimāṃ diśamaṅkayet

mīnayogyāṃ tathā yāmyāṃ saumyāśāṃ ca prasādhayet

vedāsraṃ ca catuḥsūtraṃ pātatastatra siddhyati

śāntā yaśovatī kāntā viśālā [kh: vilāsā] prāṇavāhinī

uttarāsyāṃ daśānyā ca ekāśītyaṅghrikārikā

jayā jvālā viśokā ca smṛtaitā sūtrapātataḥ

śakramarkaṃ tathā satyaṃ bhṛśaṃ vyomaṃ ca pūrvataḥ

kṛtāntamatha gāndharvaṃ bhṛṅgaṃ mṛgaṃ ca dakṣiṇe

varuṇaṃ daityaśoṣau ca yakṣmāṇaṃ paścime sadā

navāntapadago brahmā pūjyo'nye dvipadaṅghrikāḥ

tathādhaścāpavatsākhyaḥ kendrāntare tu ṣaṭpade

sāvitrī tadadho dvyaṅghre vivasvān ṣaṭpade tvadhaḥ

varuṇabrahmaṇormadhye mitrākhyaṃ ṣaṭpade yajet

ṣaḍsthaṃ] saumye dharādharam

jambhapāpapilīpicchān yaje [k, g: nyase] dīśādibāhyataḥ

śātmake]

daśakoṣṭhasthitā dikṣu tvanye ceśādikoṇagāḥ

pāpasaṃjñānilau devā sārdhasārdhāṅghrisaṃsthitāḥ

devavāstuvidhau sarvaṃ vakṣye śalyoddhṛti kramāt

[mū, ṅ: rūpavat]

sadigviṃśatkaro dairghyātsaptaviṃśativistṛtaḥ

rudradviguṇitaṃ nāhātpṛthujyotirvinā tribhiḥ

kuḍyasūtrasamā vīthī vīthībhedādanekadhā

pārśvayorvinā

vīthyardhenopavīthyāḍhya [mū: thyādya] mekadvitripurānvitam

ekaṃ yāmyaṃ ca saumyāsyaṃ dve cetpaścātpuromukham

siddhārthamambuyāmye ca indrāpye [kh: indrākhya] yamasūryakam

āpyendau gṛhacūlyākhyaṃ kācāhvaṃ yāmyasaumyayoḥ

daṇḍākhye rājato daṇḍo vātākhye kalahaḥ sadā

kh: tadbahiḥ kṛtaḥ]

yāmye hīne bhaveccūlī [mū: cchūlā] triśālaṃ vittahṛtparam

indrādikramato vacmi [mū: vahni] dhvajādyaṣṭau gṛhāṇyaham

yāmye rasakriyāśayyā varma [varcaḥ] śastrāṇi rakṣasi

saumye dhanaṃ paśūn kuryādīśe dīkṣāsurālayam

dviguṇaṃ hastasaṃyuktaṃ kṛtvāṣṭāṃśairhṛtaṃ tathā

tapaḥ pakṣāgnivedeṣu rasādyambarato bhavet

tasmācca [mū, gh: tatsyācca] navame bhāge śubhakṛnnilayo mataḥ

prāgāpye cenduyāmye vā haṭṭa [k: haste] evaṃ gṛhāvalī

īśādiditikāntāni phalānyeṣāṃ yathākramam

ṣvaṣṭakaṃ dhruvam]

śilpitvaṃ tanayaḥ syācca yāmyadvāraphalāṣṭakam

mānaśca dvārataḥ proktamuttarasyāṃ diśi kramāt

cārvībhistabhmamālābhiḥ kapāṭaiḥ sārgalairyutam

homena [ṅ: doṣa ca] tarpayetsarvān maṇḍape purato guruḥ

veśmasaṃsthāpane kāle guruṃ santoṣya bhaktitaḥ

gṛhopaskaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ dattvā śayyāsanādikam

sveṣṭadevālayaṃ kuryāttallakṣaṇamathocyate

jagatyekaṃ pṛthurvāpi [kh: pṛthgvāpi] kecitprāhuḥ surālayam

teṣāṃ madhye bhavedekaḥ karturicchāvaśena tu

pañcahasto'thavā kāryaḥ ṣaṭcaturaṅgulaḥ smṛtaḥ

viṣamaistu na [mū: mastena] mādhyāni māghyadoṣo mahānbhavet

āya [kh: āpa] doṣaviśuddhyarthaṃ dāpayettatra buddhimān

tryaṅgulaṃ pariṇāhena jinasaṅkhyāṅgulāyatam

hastadārusamāsena vyākhyātaṃ vāstusādhanam

sahasraṃ dhanuṣāṃ krośo gavyūtirdviguṇaṃ smṛtam

ekaṃ daśaśataṃ jñeyaṃ sahasramayutaṃ punaḥ

vṛndaṃ kharvaṃ nikharvaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ vai padmasaṃjñakam

parārdhaṃ sarva [kh, gh: meva] mākhyātaṃ daśavṛddhyuttarottaram

purasya sarvato dikṣu sabāhyābhyantareṣu ca

kuryādabhimukhāvaitau grāmasna nagarasya ca

śatadvaye

prākpratīcyāgato madhye vaṃśo dakṣiṇasomataḥ

paścimasaṃmukhā

nottarābhimukhāḥ śastāḥ kadāciddakṣiṇāmukhāḥ

tadādau kīrtyate sthityai kurtuḥ kīrtiśubhecchayā

hanūmadbhūtavetālamātṛkālāstu dakṣiṇe

samudrā varuṇo nadyo viśvāmitro'psaro [k, g, gh: śmaro] jale

saumye skando viśākhaśca sthāpyā yakṣā grahāstathā

purādyabhimukhā devāḥ śubhāḥ svasthānasaṃsthitā

kṛte devaṃ likhetsaumyaṃ tatkuḍrye purasammukham

svadevatāḥ]

kaṭukaṇṭakinastiktāḥ piśācākhyādipādapāḥ

prāsādasya samīpasthāḥ sarvadoṣāvahā nṛṇam

aśakta [gh: asak] cchedane teṣāṃ ropayetkṣīriṇo'ntare

nāraṅganārikelau ca panasāśca sacampakāḥ

saralonandikāvartatālāgastyatimuktakāḥ

ebhirantaritāḥ krūrā bhūruhāḥ syuḥ sukhapradāḥ

śivasya niyamo nāsti prāsādakaraṇaṃ prati

mumukṣoryatra tatreśo dadyānmuktiṃ tu helayā

pātālakhecarīsiddhiḥ śatrudāhaśca śāntayaḥ

grāmādīśendravahnisthāḥ paścimāsyāḥ surāḥ śubhāḥ

adhikaṃ vā samaṃ hinaṃ pūrvāyāmādibādhakam

ubhayodviguṇocchrāyā kuryāttyaktvāgrataḥ kṣitau

bhinnanābhau vidhiścāyaṃ na doṣo'bhinnanābhike

evaṃ bāṇamatājjñātvā harisūryādi ropayet

svayambhūte sahasraikaṃ mahadbhiḥ śatapañcakam

tatrotpannaṃ hi nirmālyaṃ mṛte jantau vimocanam

praviśya bhūmiṃ tāṃ dhīmānudagīśānagocare

dhānyakambuphalairyuktaṃ candonena sucarcitam

nyasettatra ghaṭaṃ divyaṃ tūryamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ

paraśuṃ muṣṭilāṃ sūtraṃ kīlakāṃśca taduttare

punnāgaśastavṛkṣotthāḥ kīlakāḥ ekajātayaḥ

śaṅkuṃ niveśayettasminnāja [kh: nnaja] gatyāḥ krameṇa tu

suvastrairveṣṭitāḥ sarve sudhūpenaiva dhūpitāḥ

adhomūlordhvagāgrāśca ṛjurūpāḥ suśobhanā

dakṣiṇena samutthāpya vāmahastena muṣṭinā

tāḍayaṃśca japenmantraṃ prāṅnimittāni lakṣayet

asmin gṛhe tu tiṣṭhantu āyurbalakarāḥ sadā

tataḥ pradhānakārūkastāḍayecca viśedyathā

praviśecceddrutaṃ sarvaṃ kartustatrāśubhaṃ tadā

śanaiḥ śanairadho gacchetkāryasiddhiṃ vinirdiśet

bhayamarthakṣatīrogaḥ sukhaṃ mānaṃ kramādbhavet

āyuḥ putrārthavṛddhiḥ syāccyute hastādbhayaṃ mahat

sūtraṃ niveśayacchaṅkau yathā tanna vimuñcati

tasmātsugranthitaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇena tu veṣṭayet

āgneyyāṃ tanmukho bhūtvā mantramuccārayedimam

gṛhṇantu mantritāḥ]

baliṃ tebhyaḥ prayacchāmi sarve gṛhṇantu mantritāḥ

baliṃ tebhyaḥ prayacchāmi gṛhṇantu sumanotsukāḥ

dhruvādi dīpitā mantrāḥ kumbhayitvā nivedayet

tarpitāstuṣṭamanaso bhavanti karmasiddhidāḥ

śaṅkavaḥ

nādhikaṃ sukhadaṃ karturdoṣā ete sadādhike

pravāstastaskarādbhītiḥ prāgādyādhikasūtrataḥ

raupyeṇa vā nirarthānāmartha [kh: māṣa] vṛddhayai dvijātitaḥ

tṛṇairvā [mū: tūṇairvā] likhite vāstau doṣā ete bhavanti ca

kramāt

ajinena bhavedvyādhistasmāddhemnā likhetsadā

lakṣaṇanirṇaye trayoviṃśo vidhiḥ

lipte susaṃskṛte sthāne tanyamāne [g: tatpramāṇe] ca sūtrake

a: lakṣaṇam]

mahāmarmāsthiśalyena bhayaṃ karturmahadbhavet

tasmādākarṣayecchalyaṃ tadbhavaṃ tatpramāṇataḥ

khareṇa kharajaṃ nābhyāmuccāṭaḥ syātprabhorbhayam

ajāvibhyāṃ gavāsthi syāttayorvāsthi suniścitam

aśvena māhiṣaṃ vidyā [mū, gh: vindyā] dgajenoṣṭrodbhavaṃ tathā

vyāghrāsthi śūkareṇa syāttena daṣṭe [k, kh: daṃṣṭre] riporbhayam

pāṇḍuroga sapāṇḍukaiḥ]

bhasmāṅgāreṇa dāhaḥ syātpravāsaḥ pāṃśunā drutam

sūtre santānya [kh, gh: tāḍya] māne tu vyomno viṣṭhāprapātataḥ

vṛṣabheṇa nidhānaṃ ca gomūtrādrajataṃ viduḥ

sukhāvaham

sadā

hānidam

śāntadiggajarjācca kartuścādho nidhiḥ sthitaḥ

śāntāstu phaladāśeṣā drekkāṇoccagrahodayāt

kravyānmṛgavihaṅgānāṃ kīrtanātsyāttadasthi ca

tadvṛtam]

nṛśalyāt strīcalatvaṃ syādbrahmahatyā na saṃśayaḥ

tanmadhye dvādaśeṣṭāni śeṣāṇyaniṣṭakṛnti ca

śilpino yajamānasya tvanyeṣāṃ tanniyoginām

yannāma yasya śabdaśca [k, kh: śalyaśca] śṛṇuyāttattadātmakam

niḥ śalyā tu tadā bhūmiḥ kathitavyā ca daiśikaiḥ

tasmāttaduddharecchalyaṃ tacca tasmiṃstaducyate

putrāṇāṃ maraṇaṃ tena bandhubhiḥ syādviyogitā

netrakaṇḍūyanānmuktā śrādā tadaṅgamānataḥ

tābhyāṃ hastadvayena syāddāridryaṃ maraṇaṃ kramāt

vidrumaṃ hema tāraṃ vā karṇakaṇḍūyane bhavet

grīvāyāṃ sparśane graivaṃ śṛṅkhalā vātha lohajā

tathā

aṅgaspharśādbhavedāṅgaṃ tanmānādbahurogadam

kakṣābhyāṃ kṛṣṇalohaṃ tu tanmānādduḥkhadaṃ bhṛśam

dakṣiṇe ca kare pṛṣṭhe kapālaṃ vātha mṛnmayam

vāmahaste ca saṃspṛṣṭe khaṭvāpādastadaṅgataḥ

pārśvakaṇḍūyane vidyānnarārdhena tu pāṃśulīm

udare paśuśalyaṃ syātkaṭimātre'tiśokadam

pṛṣṭhena pṛṣṭhajaṃ śalyaṃ śaukaraṃ vātha kūrparam

kaṭiṣpṛṣṭenāyaḥ kaṇṭho dvikarādho'rthanāśadaḥ

śepha [k: meha] sparśāvrilohotthaṃ karādhaḥ pramadāpaham

strīduḥśīlorujācchalyāddāruśalyāddhanāpaham

jaṅghāsparśāttadasthi syādadhaścaikādaśāṅgulāt

pādasparśādgajāsthi syātkaṇṭhaśālmalameva ca

kh: bahi] citritā

tryaṅgulībhirhayāsthi syādgomṛtiḥ sārdhatālataḥ

carma pādatalādrukmaṃ bhavedaṣṭāṅgulaiḥ sadā

gātrāṇāṃ sparśanādvidyānnānāśalyāni buddhimān

bahvaṅgasparśanānnityaṃ śalyāni vividhāni ca

śālyānivikṛtaḥ kartuścatvāriṃśacca sapta ca

khanyamāne bhuvo garte labhyante vāstuno yadi

toyāntikaṃ sthitaṃ śalyaṃ prāsāde doṣadaṃ nṛṇām

kvacidrudrasahasre tu hute doṣo na jāyate

bhittinyāse viśeṣeṇa yajedvāstuṃ trikālikam

garta [mū, gh" garbha] pramāṇataḥ samyagliptenādyānvite same

iti lakṣaṇasamuccaye śalyoddhāranirṇaye caturviṃśo vidhiḥ

ācāryaḥ sthapatiścaiva daivajño'pi guṇānvitaḥ

sthapatirviśvakarmā tu daivajño nandikeśvaraḥ

pūjayitvā tu tānsarvāṃstoṣayitvā kṣamāpayet

hiraṇyavastradānena vāgbhirvā tāṃstu toṣayet

yajñadravyāṇi saṃhṛtya viśedbhūmiṃ samāhitaḥ

ātmapūjāmarghapātraṃ kṛtvā dravyāṇi śodhayet

siñcayedambhasā bhūmiṃ tathā gandho [kh; gaṅgo] dakena ca

surūpāṃ pramadārūpāṃ śuddhālaṅkārabhūṣitām

sarvadevamayaṃ vāstupuruṣaṃ tatra pūjayet

ghaṭikāśaṅkuyogena nakṣatradarśanena vā

suśobhā bhānukī cetāḥ prāṅmukhyo navanāḍikāḥ

vidrumā vibhavetyetā uttarāsyā navoditāḥ

kṛtvā yajeddharādīnāṃ nava [mū, a: bhava] tyardhaṃ divaukasām

toyotpalaiśca parjanyaṃ jayaṃ piṣṭa [gh: yajeddiṣṭaṃ] patākayā

kṣaudraṃ gṛhakṣatāyātha caṇakānnaṃ yamāya ca

sugrīvāya dvayoryāvī puṣpadantāya darbhakam

ghṛtāmbu ketave dhānāḥ paṅkaverddhe tu [kh, gh: vetra] paścime

kṣayāya] ca

dityai lopīmadityai ca pūpā [kh, ṅ: sūpā] nardhe'tha saumyataḥ

tadadhaḥ koṣṭhake dadyādāpavatsāya vai dadhi

agnyadhaḥ koṣṭhake nityaṃ sāvitryai [kh, g: savitre] ca kuśāmbhasā

vivasvate'ruṇāṃ dadyāccandanaṃ caturaṅghriṣu

indrajayāya tasyādho ghṛtānnaṃ koṇakoṣṭhake

vāṭvadhaḥ koṇadeśe tu rudrāya pakṣimāṃsakam

mahīdharāyamāṣānnaṃ samāṃsaṃ caturaṅghriṣu

etānvastrādibhiśceṣṭvā tato rakṣo'ṣṭakaṃ kramāt

pūtanāṃ palapittābhyāṃ māṃsāsṛgbhyāṃ ca jambhakam

rakṣomātṛgaṇeśebhyaḥ piśācādi eva ca

asantarpyāpyaniṣṭvaitān prāsādādīnnakārayet

purotpanno mahāvīryaḥ sarvabhūtabhayāvahaḥ

yāvadūrvīsthirā loke tāvadvāstunaraḥ sthitaḥ

sarveṣu vāstukāryeṣu pūjayedvāstunāyakam

vistāro'sya samantācca śatakoṭistu yojanaiḥ

īśakoṇe śirastasya aṃśo bāhuśca pūrvataḥ

upabāhūruhastaśca pārśvaścaivāgnideśataḥ

kuḍyādirohaṇe kāle mūrdhādīni na pīḍayet

etadvaṃśa [mū, a: ttvaṃśa] gatā nāḍya upavaṃśagatāsvimāḥ

sāvitrī savitā tadvadupabāhau karau hṛdi

pañjikāḥ]

vāstudevatā

mūrdhāsyahṛtstanaunābhirmahāmarmāṇi pañca ca

viniveśitāḥ

tūryamaṅgalanirghoṣairnimittaiḥ śobhanaiḥ śubhaiḥ

anyāḥ krameṇa nyastavyāḥ svadiksthā mantriṇā tataḥ

liṅgavatsadā

vasānataḥ (?)

nārdhamānataḥ]

aghamā cetareṣāṃ tu śivasya tu yathecchayā

tūryamaṅgalaśabdena prāsādaṃ tatra sūtrayet

vidhiḥ

ardhena liṅgadairghyeṇa bhittayo vistṛtāḥ kramāt

tadaṃśatulyavīthyastu syurarkāṃśaistu bhittayaḥ

tadgarbho'ṣṭābhiraṃśaiḥ syādvikārāṃśaistu bhittayaḥ

kaniṣṭhādikramādgarbhaḥ kvacidbhittestu vardhanam

garbhavyomasya cārkeṇa pīṭhī madhye yugāṃśataḥ

dviguṇaṃ tat kvacitproktaṃ prāsāde rasabhājite

jyeṣṭhādyā nirgamā jñeyā nemī tatpādavarjitā

yugāṅghrermadhyato garbhaḥ syāttadekena piṇḍikā

aṣṭāviṃśatibhirbhittiḥ sā syāt stambhakumārikā

catuḥ stāmbhāśca tanmadhye dvādaśa syuḥ kumārikāḥ

pārśvayo ragratā vāpi kuryādāsārasaṃyute

sakoṇastambhasaṃyukto maṇḍapaśca tridhā mataḥ

dvādalāṃśaistu tān sarvān prāsāde cottamodike

tathānyataḥ

jagatīmaṃśato dhṛtvāveṣṭanāṃśaṃ tu lopayet

vidikṣu koṇadhāmāni vasvaṃśairmaṇḍapaiḥ saha

samasūtrāṅkake chede samabhāgādvivartayet

cāru surālayastambhabalabhīśikharāṇi ca

vāstumānamiti khyātaṃ liṅgamānamathocyate

mañjarī tu dvibhāgābhyāṃ mañjaryardhena nāsikā

pañcāṃśaistatpṛthutvaṃ ca tathordhvastryaṃśakairgalaḥ

kramādgrīvādikocchrayaḥ

dhāmārdhātbhramaṇa kāryā tadardhena ca bhittayaḥ

maṇḍapārdhapramāṇena purato mukhamaṇḍapaḥ

catuṣkikāgrato nityaṃ jagatī dhāmamānataḥ

tatsamā balabhī diksthāḥ svamānamaṇḍapānvitāḥ

prāsādaturyabhāgena tryaṃśairvā tatsamucchrayam

taducchrayaṃ haraṃ kṛtvā sādhāṃśe khura [gh: kṣura] paṭṭikā

'śaṃguṇaṃ bhavet

tṛtīyenopapaṭṭī tu tapāṃśā [kh: tathāṅgā] ccaturasrikā

taddhyaṃśamaṣṭadhā kṛtvā taiśca padmaṃ vivartayet

karṇikā śaśibhāgena vistārāccādhunocyate

tanmadhye tu dvibhāgābhyāṃ praveśo gṛha [g: graha] hetave

evameva tu jaṅghāyāmañjaryā vedikāvadhiḥ

prāsādaśikharaṃ vāpi brūyāmuparathādikān

pīṭhādyabjāntamucchrāye praveśaśca nigadyate

ardhena paṭṭikāṃ kuryāddvābhyāṃ kaṇṭhapraveśakam

ardhāṃśena tadūrdhve tu kāryā caivopapaṭṭikā

tryāṅghripraveśo haṃsa [gh: dvyaṃśa] statpaṭṭyardhapraveśataḥ

sandhipatrānvitaṃ [kh: ṅkita] padmaṃ vidadhīta suśobhanam

śilotthaṃ padapīṭhaṃ syādevaṃ dārviṣṭakāmaye

taducchrayaṃ grahaṃ kṛtvā jaṅghā tanmadhyato guṇaiḥ

vedibandhavaraṇḍā [mū, a: vavaṇḍā] dinnigadāmyanupūrvaśaḥ

ucchraye dhāmaturyāṃśāttasminnidrāṃśakekṛte

sārdhatrayeṇa kumbhaḥ syāttadardhe kumbhapaṭṭikā

bhāgārdhena tu kaṇṭhaḥ syātkalaśaśca guṇāṃśataḥ

praveśāccaikataḥ kaṇṭhāścārdhe [mū: sārdhe] nirgamapaṭṭikā

kumbhapaṭṭī tu pādena kupālyā dyaṃśataḥ sthitā

tribhiḥ khurādibhiryuktaṃ vedībandhābhidhānakam

kṛteṣu triṣu bhāgeṣu pādonāṃśātpraveśikān

bhāgena caturasrākhyā paṭṭikā sārdhataḥ sthitā

khuṃrakumbhavatī jaṅghā jyeṣṭhānāṃ pādamānataḥ

yāvaccinnavakāṃ vāpi patravalyāthavā varā

caturthāṃśaśca jaṅghordhve vibhajya vasubhāgataḥ

tatrārdhena praveśākhyaṃ paṭṭikāṃ tu vi [kh: bhuvi] vartayet

pādonāṃśena haṃsākhyā [kh; hastākhyā] paṭṭī tatpadato bhavet

tatpaṭṭī pādataḥ kāryā pādonāṃśena nirgamā

bhāgamekaṃ tridhā kṛtvā praveśaścaika bhāgataḥ

paṭṭikā tu prakartavyā dvitīyenopanirgamā

balitāhvaṃ tribhi [kh: ṅkāstribhi] rbhāgairgrahāṃśaistu kaṭirbhavet

kaṇṭhasūtrādviśetkaṭho bhāgāttatpaṭṭikordhvaḥ [mū: kārdhataḥ]

bhūmerjaṅghānitambau ca kīrtitau tvaṣṭṭa śāstrataḥ

tadadhaḥ sārdhapakṣāṃśaṃ vistāreṇa tu kārayet

ekaikena tu bhāgega viśetkaṇṭhaka [kh, ṅ: kaṃ kaṇṭha] sūtrataḥ

bhavedanurathaḥ ṣadbhiḥ saptamenodakāntaram

koṇānurathakādīnāṃ vṛttapraveśa ucyate

tadvaccaturthabhāgena praveśo'nurathasya ca

luptvā bhāgān bhuvāṃ svāṃ svāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca yathākramam

pādonadvyaṃśataḥ kumbhastatpaṭṭī pādato bhavet

kumbhopame ca te kārye churikāpakṣayostathā

pādena kumbhapaṭṭī tu cārdhena galapaṭṭikā

kumbhārdhena tathā paṭṭī kaṇṭhastatpaṭṭīkārdhataḥ

trikaṇṭhakocchrayaṃ dvābhyāṃ pakṣayostatra vartayet

mastaṃ] lavalīphalavadbhavet

nirgamākhyā parāṃśena [gh: parārdhena] caturasrāṃśato bhavet

tanmadhye tilakaṃ kāryamevaṃ tryaṃśairvaraṇḍikā

rūpaṃ koṇarathasyaivaṃ vakṣye coparathaṃ tvataḥ

kumbhaikenārdhataḥ paṭṭī kaṇṭhaścārdhāṃśatastathā

vṛttapaṭṭī ca niṣkrāntā bhāgabhāgena kalpayet

aṃśe gartālakaṃ tajña tatpārśvayorguṇāṃśataḥ

ūrdhvādhastilakākrāntā madhyasthānordhvataḥ sthitā

caturbhāgaiścaturbhāgaiḥ śeṣānyāścandraśālikā

evaṃ pañcadaśākhyātāḥ pañcabhūmīnduśālikāḥ

sārdhena madhyatastatra rathakārdhaṃ samudgatas

ṣaṣṭibhāgasamucchāyaṃ kuryādardhenduśālikām

kumbhapaṭṭīṃ tathā kaṇṭhamardhena parikalpayet

tathā kāryā bhāgabhāgena nirmitā

khaneśca tilakopetaṃ tadbāhye'rdhenduśālikā

tanmadhye śikharī dvyaṃśā ucchritātimanoramā

tatpārśvārdhārathe'pyevaṃ kartavyārdhendaśālikā

uparyupari ca trīṇi ekaṃ [mū, a, kh, g, ṅ: evaṃ] bhūsūtratulyataḥ

madhyarathasya karmedaṃ vedikādyamathocyate

pādātpraveśapaṭṭī syānnirgamā paṭṭikāṅghritaḥ

haṃsākhyāntārdhasūtrācca ṣaḍvibhāgastaducchrayaḥ

kapolādaṃśaniṣkāśādamalāsāravistaraḥ

ekena caturasrā syād dvābhyāṃ haṃsakapotikā

sabhūtāṃśaistribhiḥ kāryo ghaṭastaddviguṇocchrayaḥ

kuryād bhūmiṃ samaṃ ramyaṃ susthitaṃ jalapūritam

dravyaṃ kālānurūpeṇa yojayedbhāgasammitam

grīyme nu pañcabhāgāḥ syuḥ śītakāle tathā trayaḥ

evaṃ dattvā tu yaḥ pūrvaṃ bandho'tra kathitaḥ kṣiteḥ

pavitrārohaṇaṃ [mū: ropaṇaṃ] kāryaṃ cāturmāsyaṃ vrataṃ caran

sthaṇḍile hariśambhostu dale kesarakoṇake

sūtraiśca granthayo'nyeṣāṃ tathā daśaika [g: dalaika] hānitaḥ

itareṣāṃ tu devānāṃ pavitraṃ syāt trisūtrakam [gh: trakaiḥ]

kvacidvitastimātraṃ ca śatārdhārdhārdhasūtrakam [kh, gh: trakaiḥ]

dadyāt pavitrakaṃ tadvadvahnyādidikṣu cetaraiḥ

tripañcasūtrakairvāpi hṛdā cāmantrarṇa vibhoḥ [k, kh, ṅ: prabhoḥ]

gṛhāṇemāṃ mahādeva samastavidhikāraṇa

pañcagavyaṃ kamātteṣu tālacandranalohakam

homadravyāṇi sarvāṇi sakuśānyaparāparānane

tāmbūlaṃ guhyake dadyāt sājyaṃ caru nare punaḥ

aiśānyāmāsanaṃ chatraṃ pāduke yogapaṭṭakam

pātraṃ kṛtvā pavitrāṇi prokṣitāni śivāmbhasā

gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpādyaṃ sākṣataṃ ca pavitrakam

śivaṃ nivedayenmūrdhni recakenāmṛtīkṛtam

prātaḥ snātaḥ śuciḥ śuddhaḥ pūjāṃ kuryātsvavittataḥ

pañcabhirmadhuraiḥ snānaṃ jalāntarairvirukṣaṇam

aṣṭāṅgo'rghaṃ pradāyātha samālabhya prapūjayet

vastraiḥ saṃchāditaṃ veśma ghaṭācāmarabhūṣaṇaiḥ

kadalīcūtamālābhirjātabījasarocakaiḥ [k, kh, g, gh, ṅ: sarāvakaiḥ]
